### [112] [The Preprint Evolution: The Rise of Unreviewed Drafts on arXiv and its Implications for Astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35787)
*Luigi Rolly Bedin*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文分析了arXiv在天文学领域因预印本激增导致的认知负担与学术生态问题，提出将提交分为两级：Level-1（已同行评审）和Level-2（预审草稿），以减轻负担、保障研究自由与公平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arXiv早期促进了天文学研究的民主化，但当前海量未审核预印本造成认知过载、干扰盲审、损害青年学者独立研究能力。

Method: 分析十年astro-ph元数据，量化趋势；提出双轨制投稿模型（Level-1与Level-2）。

Result: 揭示预印本泛滥带来的冗余工作、虚假优先权及对早期职业研究者的负面影响；论证双轨制可显著降低阅读负担并恢复评审公正性。

Conclusion: 应实施分级提交机制，区分已审与未审内容，以保护研究人员时间与学术自主权。

Abstract: The arXiv preprint server originally democratized astronomical research by digitizing the circulation of early manuscripts and removing the financial barriers of journal subscriptions. However, a system designed for the lower publication volumes of the 1990s has fractured under modern research output. Today, the sheer volume of unreviewed manuscripts---often tagged as ``submitted,'', ``under review'', or ``comments welcome''---imposes a severe cognitive ``tax'' on the community. Researchers are forced to track multiple, unvetted versions of the same work, creating a massive redundant workload. This practice also bypasses the blinded peer-review process by establishing premature precedence, and it takes a heavy toll on early-career researchers. The pressure to rapidly post unvetted results stifles independent research, pushing young scientists toward massive mainstream collaborations where individual creativity gets lost. In this Short Communication, I analyze a decade of astro-ph metadata to quantify this trend. To safeguard both researchers' time and research freedom, I propose bifurcating submissions into two separate streams. Level-1 (peer-reviewed science) would host peer-reviewed science (accompanied by a journal acceptance or final DOI), while Level-2 (pre-review drafts) would be reserved for discussion drafts. This distinction would remove the unfair advantage of establishing false precedence through drafts and drastically cut the daily reading burden by allowing researchers to opt out of tracking unvetted versions.

</details>


### [113] [Reconstructing Implicit Scientific Knowledge: Evaluating LLM Agents through End-to-End Reproduction of Astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35900)
*Yuehui Wang,Xinyu Qi,Guirong Xue,Cheng Wang,Yangbin Xie,Xiaoyu Tang,Cong Sun*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过端到端复现评估大语言模型（LLM）在科学推理重建中能力的框架，发现论文中大量隐含方法学信息导致复现路径不唯一；在天文学案例中，13篇Nature论文中有11篇存在此类歧义，敏感性分析显示同一量值可因不同处理路径产生显著差异（2.16–3.53 kpc），而匹配发表结果并不等价于成功重建推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs正加速融入科研流程，但其重建已发表研究背后隐含推理的能力尚未被探索；论文常省略关键方法学细节（如数据选择、校准修正、先验假设等），导致复现失败难以归因于模型缺陷还是原文表述不清。

Method: 提出一个端到端复现评估框架，将执行与验证分离，区分计算失败与方法学歧义；对14篇天文学论文（1篇ApJ + 13篇Nature）进行实证分析，并在控制案例中开展3×2×2敏感性分析（样本定义、天区掩模、视差零点处理）。

Result: 13篇Nature论文中11篇存在阻碍唯一复现路径的方法学歧义；敏感性分析生成12条路径，所得距离估计值范围为2.16–3.53 kpc，仅1条路径复现出文中的2.70 kpc；关键信息（+0.02 mas视差零点修正）虽已在原文中，但LLM未能识别其因果相关性，直到分析揭示其影响。

Conclusion: 仅匹配发表结果不能验证LLM对科学推理的真正重建能力；瓶颈常在于无法关联相关信息，而非无法检索；端到端复现既是可重复性检验，也是评估AI系统对隐性科学知识掌握程度的有效框架。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into scientific workflows is accelerating, yet their ability to reconstruct the reasoning underlying published research remains unexplored. Papers specify explicit procedures while leaving many methodological dependencies-data selection, calibration corrections, priors, and domain assumptions-implicit. This ambiguity complicates the evaluation of LLM-based agents, since a failure to reproduce a result may reflect either limitations of the agent or underspecification in the source. We present a framework that evaluates agents through end-to-end reproduction, separating execution from verification and computational failure from methodological ambiguity. We apply it to fourteen astronomy studies: a case study from The Astrophysical Journal and thirteen papers published in Nature. Eleven of the thirteen contained an ambiguity preventing a uniquely specified reproduction path. In a controlled case study, twelve predefined paths, a 3x2x2 sensitivity analysis over sample definition, sky masking, and parallax zero-point treatment-gave estimates from 2.16 to 3.53 kpc for the same quantity, with only one recovering the published value (about 2.70 kpc). The published value was never used as an optimization target, selection criterion, or stopping condition; the matching path was found only after all twelve had run. Crucially, the decisive information (a +0.02 mas parallax zero-point correction) was already in the paper, but the agents did not recognize its causal relevance until the analysis made the effect visible. Matching a published outcome therefore does not validate reconstruction of the underlying reasoning, and the bottleneck is as often a failure to connect relevant information as to retrieve it. End-to-end reproduction thus serves both as a test of reproducibility and as a framework for evaluating implicit scientific knowledge in AI systems.

</details>


### [114] [SIFARI: Self-Supervised Interferometric Fitting for Astronomical Radio Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35966)
*Shunyuan Mao,Andrea Isella,Paris Perdikaris,Li-Ta Lo,Hui Li*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出SIFARI，一种自监督神经网络方法，用于射电干涉成像，无需外部训练集或显式空间正则化，能更精细地重建图像、提供不确定性估计，并在合成与真实ALMA数据测试中优于传统CLEAN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CLEAN方法在空间滤波、复杂形态重建和不确定性量化方面存在局限；而其他直接拟合可见度的方法又常依赖人工设定先验和超参数。

Method: SIFARI将天空亮度建模为位置的连续函数，采用自监督神经网络直接拟合观测可见度；通过经验法则设置傅里叶特征尺度以控制细节拟合能力；使用SWAG采样网络权重以估计亮度不确定性，并结合热噪声底限生成空间分辨信噪比图。

Result: 在合成ALMA测试中，SIFARI点源响应宽度约为自然加权CLEAN恢复波束的1/8，缺失短基线时恢复更多延展流量，且在三类形态基准测试中图像保真度均高于CLEAN；应用于PDS 70和WISPIT 2真实ALMA数据时，成功恢复环状结构与中心致密辐射，并支持相位自校准，使最终图像RMS噪声降低约30%。

Conclusion: SIFARI是一种无需训练数据、无需人工正则化、可量化不确定性的新型射电干涉成像方法，在分辨率、灵敏度与鲁棒性方面显著优于传统CLEAN，适用于多样化天文观测场景。

Abstract: Radio-interferometric images are reconstructed from sparsely sampled visibilities, and CLEAN-based imaging can struggle with spatial filtering, complex morphologies, and uncertainty quantification. Alternative methods that fit visibilities directly can address some of these limitations but often require manual choices of image priors and model hyperparameters. We present SIFARI (Self-Supervised Interferometric Fitting for Astronomical Radio Imaging), a self-supervised neural network workflow that represents sky brightness as a continuous function of position and fits measured visibilities without an external image training set or explicit spatial regularizer. An empirical rule sets the Fourier feature scale from the visibilities before training, controlling how readily the network fits fine structure. Sampling network weights with Stochastic Weight Averaging-Gaussian (SWAG) gives approximate brightness uncertainty estimates, which we combine with a thermal-noise floor to construct spatially resolved signal-to-noise maps. In synthetic ALMA tests, SIFARI yields an effective point-source response about eight times narrower than the natural-weighting CLEAN restoring beam and recovers more extended flux than CLEAN when short baselines are missing. It also achieves higher image fidelity than the restored CLEAN images in all three morphology benchmarks. Applied to ALMA observations of PDS 70, SIFARI recovers the bright outer ring together with faint compact emission in the central cavity. For long-baseline-only WISPIT 2 data, SIFARI supplies a sky model for phase self-calibration where the CLEAN model is inadequate. The restored, self-calibrated SIFARI image has approximately 30% lower RMS noise than the CLEAN image made from the original visibilities without self-calibration.

</details>


### [115] [Improved Modeling for Moving Sources of Radio Frequency Interference and Impact on Flagging Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35983)
*Jade M. Ducharme,Jonathan C. Pober,Michael J. Wilensky*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了移动射频干扰（RFI）对低频21-cm宇宙学观测的影响，建立了其在uv平面上产生sinc型调制的解析模型，并通过MWA数据和模拟验证；比较了多种RFI标记策略在功率谱恢复中的性能，发现按时间步全频段标记对强或频繁RFI最有效，但尚无法完全恢复无污染谱，需进一步建模减除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动RFI（如飞机、卫星）在低频21-cm宇宙学观测（尤其是再电离时期EoR）中造成严重干扰，其因相关器有限积分时间而在uv平面产生非均匀sinc型调制，传统标记方法难以兼顾弱污染与uv-mode损失，亟需系统分析与优化。

Method: 建立移动RFI在uv平面产生sinc型响应的解析模型；利用真实MWA观测、数值模拟及可控RFI注入实验进行定性验证；在注入污染的干净MWA数据上对比多种flagging策略（如逐基线标记、基线聚合标记、全频段时间步标记）对EoR功率谱恢复的影响。

Result: 发现标记策略效果依赖RFI亮度与占空比；对明亮或高频次RFI，标记所有频率对应污染时间步最能一致抑制多余功率；但所有测试策略均未能完全恢复无污染参考功率谱。

Conclusion: 当前flagging方法存在根本局限，需发展针对移动RFI的直接建模与减除技术，以实现高保真EoR 21-cm信号探测。

Abstract: Radio frequency interference (RFI) is a major challenge for low-frequency radio experiments targeting the redshifted 21-cm signal from the Epoch of Reionization. Many important RFI sources, including aircraft and satellites, move across the sky during an observation. The averaging of visibilities for such moving sources over a finite correlator integration produces a distinct sinc-like pattern in the interferometric uv-plane, causing the RFI to appear bright on some baselines and strongly suppressed on others. Here, we develop an analytical model for this effect and validate its qualitative behavior against real MWA observations and numerical simulations. Controlled RFI injections into clean MWA data are then used to compare several flagging treatments in the context of EoR power spectrum recovery. These treatments explore the trade-off between per-baseline flagging, which can miss weak but potentially non-negligible contamination on baselines where the integration effect leads to strong suppression, and baseline-aggregated flagging, which removes more faint residual contamination but reduces the total number of usable uv-modes. We find that the preferred strategy depends on RFI brightness and occupancy, but that flagging all frequencies at contaminated time-steps most consistently minimizes excess power for bright or frequent events. However, none of the tested flagging treatments fully recovers the uncontaminated reference spectrum, motivating future work on direct modeling and subtraction of moving RFI sources.

</details>


### [116] [Divergence-free magnetohydrodynamics on a moving mesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35989)
*Volker Springel,Alexander C. Mayer,Rüdiger Pakmor*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于矢量势的、在非结构移动网格上实现无散度且伽利略不变的理想磁流体动力学（MHD）数值方法，通过特定规范选择和离散积分方案，在AREPO代码中实现了稳定、高精度的MHD模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想MHD数值模拟面临磁场散度误差导致的数值不稳定与物理不一致问题；现有方法难以兼顾无散度性与伽利略不变性，尤其在拉格朗日型代码中缺乏满足这两者的成熟方案。

Method: 采用单元中心有限差分法演化矢量势（A），结合有限体积法表示其余流体变量；在移动网格（AREPO）中引入特定规范（如Weyl规范）与适配的离散时间积分格式，确保磁场∇·B=0且整体伽利略不变。

Result: 该方法在场环对流等经典测试中散度误差为零，展现出优异的稳定性与精度；成功应用于含局部时间步、自适应网格加密/粗化（AMR）的三维实际天体物理问题。

Conclusion: 基于矢量势与恰当规范的移动网格MHD方法，首次在拉格朗日框架下同时实现了严格的无散度性和伽利略不变性，为高保真宇宙学与星系演化模拟提供了可靠新工具。

Abstract: Magnetic fields are ubiquitous in the Universe and play an important role in many astrophysical processes, such as star formation and the evolution of galaxies. Hydrodynamical simulation methods that account for ideal magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) are however challenged by the need to achieve numerical stability and physical consistency. Both can be compromised by the occurrence of non-vanishing local divergence errors in the magnetic field. Different approaches to either eliminate or reduce these discretization errors are in widespread use, including constrained transport or divergence cleaning techniques. However, thus far a divergence-free Galilean invariant formulation of MHD that can be readily applied in Lagrangian codes has been an elusive goal. Here we revisit the use of the vector potential for evolving the magnetic field on an unstructured moving-mesh and show how a particular gauge choice combined with a suitably discretized integration scheme provides a stable and accurate formulation of MHD. Our method combines a finite-difference evolution of a cell-centred vector potential with a finite volume representation of the other fluid quantities, and is realized in the moving-mesh code AREPO. The new approach is fully Galilean invariant and therefore shows vanishing errors for classic problems such as field loop advection. We demonstrate good accuracy and stability when our scheme is applied to various test problems and to practical applications in 3D, including when local timesteps and adaptive mesh refinement and derefinement are used.

</details>


### [117] [Comparing Active Anomaly Detection Frameworks: From Light Curves to Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36096)
*Sreevarsha Sreejith,Robert C. Nichol*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种主动异常检测（A-AD）框架（Astronomaly、Protege、PineForest）在天文时序与图像数据中的性能，发现其效果依赖于先验样本构成和数据类型；通过混合Protege与PineForest，在10,000张Euclid图像中高效发现了799个形态异常天体，包括新强引力透镜候选体和环状星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代天文巡天产生海量复杂数据，人工全面目视检查已不现实，亟需能兼顾科学目标导向与意外发现能力的自动化异常检测方法。

Method: 系统评估三种主动异常检测（A-AD）框架（Astronomaly、Protege、PineForest）在ZTF光变曲线和Euclid星系图像上的表现；开展基线实验、带先验标签实验，并构建Protege与PineForest的混合搜索策略。

Result: Protege和PineForest在ZTF光变数据中前100个样本分别检出83和78颗变星；PineForest在Euclid图像中前100张检出70个并合/形态扰动星系，显著优于其他两者；混合策略在10,000张Euclid图像中发现799个形态异常目标，含29个强引力透镜（其中9个为新候选体）及202个环状星系。

Conclusion: A-AD方法的选择应匹配具体科学目标与数据特性；Protege与PineForest互补性强，混合策略可显著提升对罕见天体的发现效率，为未来大规模巡天提供高效、可扩展的发现范式。

Abstract: The increasing volume and complexity of data from modern astronomical surveys are making comprehensive visual inspection increasingly impractical, motivating automated methods for identifying rare and unusual objects. Active anomaly detection (A-AD) combines unsupervised anomaly detection with iterative user feedback, allowing searches to adapt to scientific interests while retaining sensitivity to unexpected objects. We systematically compare three A-AD frameworks, Astronomaly, Protege, and PineForest, across Zwicky Transient Facility light curves and Euclid galaxy images. In baseline experiments, Protege, PineForest, and Astronomaly recovered 83, 78, and 38 variable stars, respectively, within the first 100 light-curve objects inspected, while PineForest recovered 70 merging or morphologically disturbed galaxies within the first 100 Euclid images, compared with 29 and 19 for Astronomaly and Protege. Experiments using prior labels show that their effectiveness depends on the composition of the prior sample and varies between A-AD methods. Finally, we combine the complementary behaviour of Protege and PineForest in a hybrid search of 10,000 Euclid images, recovering 799 morphologically unusual objects. These include 29 retained strong lens systems and candidates, of which 9 candidates have no counterparts in the published Euclid Q1 strong lens catalogues searched, and 202 visually classified ring galaxies. These results highlight the importance of selecting A-AD strategies according to both the scientific objective and dataset properties, and demonstrate their potential for efficient, discovery-driven exploration of forthcoming astronomical surveys.

</details>


### [118] [ECLIPSE-X: Plate-Scale Calibration as the Binding Constraint on Relativistic Astrometry with Spacecraft Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36514)
*Pedro J. Llanos*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出ECLIPSE-X架构，用于在太阳附近通过恒星天体测量联合估计PPN参数γ与航天器姿态，发现未建模的像面尺度误差是导致统计失效的主要原因，需引入尺度参数进行校准以保证协方差有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在真实天体测量模型误差下，联合估计PPN参数γ与航天器导航状态的解及其形式协方差是否保持统计有效性，而非追求γ的高精度测定。

Method: 构建ECLIPSE-X局部多帧估计架构，将持久性相对论参数γ与帧依赖的姿态参数（视线方向和滚动角）联合估计；利用其空间特征差异与γ的跨帧持久性进行分离；采用加权最小二乘估计，并通过Schur补边缘化获得γ的形式不确定性；通过1000次仿真验证标定效果并分析各类未建模扰动的影响。

Result: 名义解得γ = 1.0001198167，σ_γ = 3.075×10⁻⁴；像面尺度误差（300 ppm）导致经验/形式不确定度比高达7126.6，覆盖率降为零；而星表坐标失配和径向扰动影响较小；引入尺度参数可恢复协方差一致性，但使γ精度下降21.2%；未增强估计器仅在尺度误差标准差<2.7884×10⁻⁸时协方差一致。

Conclusion: 对于近太阳相对论天体测量估计器，引入像面尺度参数是保证统计有效性的必要条件，而非可选改进；数值收敛不等于统计有效，必须显式建模主导系统误差。

Abstract: Precision stellar astrometry near the Sun can constrain the parametrized post-Newtonian (PPN) parameter gamma through gravitational light deflection. This study develops ECLIPSE-X, a localized multi-frame estimation architecture in which one persistent relativistic parameter is estimated simultaneously with frame-dependent spacecraft line-of-sight and roll states, separated by their differing spatial signatures and by the persistence of gamma across frames. The aim is not a competitive determination of gamma, but an assessment of whether such a joint solution and its formal covariance stay statistically valid under realistic astrometric model error. A synthetic field of 250 stars spanning 1.22-8 apparent solar radii is observed over 40 frames at 5-s cadence; weighted least squares estimates gamma with three navigation states per frame, and Schur-complement marginalization gives the formal uncertainty in gamma after navigation coupling. The nominal solution recovers gamma = 1.0001198167 with sigma_gamma = 3.075104e-4, and a 1000-realization ensemble confirms calibration. Perturbations unknown to the estimator identify plate-scale mismatch as the dominant failure mechanism: a 300-ppm scale error alone inflates the empirical-to-formal uncertainty ratio to 7126.6 with zero 1-sigma and 2-sigma coverage, against 2.18 for catalog-coordinate mismatch and 1.12 for a frame-coherent radial perturbation, while every realization converges numerically: convergence does not guarantee statistical validity. Augmenting the state with a plate-scale parameter restores consistency, at a fixed 21.2% cost in the precision of gamma set by the correlation between the two persistent parameters. A calibration sweep shows the unaugmented estimator is covariance-consistent only below sigma_p = 2.7884e-8, so the augmented state is a requirement, not a refinement, for near-Sun relativistic astrometric estimators.

</details>


### [119] [LUMA: A CNN for Strong Gravitational Lens Searches in Astronomical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36857)
*Giovanni Vincenzo Donatiello,Achille A. Nucita,Francesco De Paolis,Antonio Franco,Francesco Strafella*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: LUMA是一个用于自动检测强引力透镜的卷积神经网络（CNN）流程，结合物理启发的预处理和轻量级CNN，在模拟数据上达到96%准确率和0.99 ROC AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升强引力透镜在天文图像中的自动化检测能力，尤其针对微弱弧形与环状特征；为后续应用于真实巡天图像及更先进模型（如Transformer）奠定基础。

Method: 提出LUMA：包含物理驱动的预处理（增强微弱透镜特征）和紧凑三模块CNN；采用类别重加权与现代学习率调度策略进行训练。

Result: 在模拟数据上测试准确率达约96%，非平凡类ROC AUC≈0.99；混淆矩阵分析显示高完备性与高纯度。

Conclusion: 轻量级CNN架构可作为强透镜搜索的有效基线，具备向真实观测图像及Transformer模型拓展的潜力。

Abstract: We present LUMA, a convolutional neural network (CNN) pipeline for the automated detection of strong gravitational lenses in simulated astronomical imaging. The method combines a physically motivated preprocessing stage, which enhances faint arc and ring features, with a compact three-block CNN trained using class reweighting and modern learning-rate scheduling. On simulated data, the model reaches test accuracies of about $96$\% and receiver operating characteristic (ROC) area-under-the-curve (AUC) values of $\simeq 0.99$ for the non-trivial classes, while confusion-matrix analysis shows high completeness and purity for lens candidates. These results demonstrate that relatively lightweight CNN architectures can provide a competitive baseline for strong-lens searches, and they motivate future extensions toward real survey images and transformer-based models.

</details>


### [120] [First laboratory demonstration of iEFC for high-contrast imaging at small angular separation on ELT scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37120)
*Charles Sallard,Patrice Martinez,Alain Spang,Kian Milani,Lyu Abe,Mathilde Beaulieu,Carole Gouvret,Aurélie Marcotto,Julien Dejonghe,Olivier Preis*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文首次在实验室条件下，利用SPEED测试平台，在分段式孔径望远镜上成功将隐式电场共轭（iEFC）技术应用于极小内工作角（1–4 λ/D）的暗区（DH）构建，验证了其在极大望远镜（ELT）高对比度成像中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足极大望远镜（ELT）级仪器对极高对比度和极小内工作角（IWA）的严苛要求，需发展适用于分段式孔径、小IWA、高对比度成像的波前控制技术。

Method: 采用隐式电场共轭（iEFC）这一模型无关的波前控制方法，在SPEED高对比度测试平台上（配备APCMC小IWA日冕仪及多平面/离瞳变形镜DM架构）开展实验，针对H波段、分段式孔径进行暗区构建。

Result: 首次实现1–4 λ/D极小内工作角的暗区；证实离瞳、大间距DM可支持低至1 λ/D的IWA；验证iEFC与SPEED架构结合在分段式望远镜上的高对比度成像有效性。

Conclusion: iEFC与SPEED专用架构协同可有效支撑ELT级仪器在衍射极限附近开展系外行星直接成像，为未来超高分辨率系外行星探测奠定关键技术基础。

Abstract: Implicit electric field conjugation (iEFC) is an efficient, model-free wavefront control technique for dark hole creation. Its model-free nature, relying on calibration with real data rather than a synthetic model of the instrument, makes it uniquely versatile and highly attractive. It has been successfully demonstrated in a variety of environments, both in the laboratory and on-sky, using different experimental platforms and coronagraphs. A common characteristic of these demonstrations is that the resulting DHs exhibit moderate to large inner working angles and wide fields of view, often extending over several tens of $λ/D$. Here, we report the first demonstration of iEFC at small angular separations, achieving a DH spanning 1 to 4 $λ/D$ on a segmented pupil in H-band under laboratory conditions. This combination of aggressive IWA and reduced FoV highlights the uniqueness of the SPEED facility (segmented pupil experiment for exoplanet detection). SPEED is a high-contrast imaging testbed designed for extremely large telescopes (ELTs), developed in the context of the ELT/PCS instrument (planetary camera and spectrograph), currently in pre-phase A. Its small-IWA coronagraph, an APCMC (apodized pupil complex mask coronagraph), and its multi- and out-of-pupil-plane deformable mirror (DM) architecture are specifically optimized for DHs at very small IWAs, down to the diffraction limit. Our results demonstrate (i) that widely separated out-of-pupil DMs are well suited to generating DHs with IWAs down to 1 $λ/D$, and (ii) the effectiveness of iEFC, when combined with the SPEED wavefront control architecture, for high-contrast imaging with segmented telescopes. Ultimately, these results pave the way toward meeting the stringent high-contrast requirements of ELT-class instruments, opening new opportunities for exoplanet detection at unprecedented angular resolutions.

</details>


### [121] [On-sky commissioning and technical performance of Proto-NUX at Pic du Midi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37765)
*Rasjied Sloot,Rudy Wijnands,Steven Bloemen,Hans Ellermeijer,Alexander Hoogerbrug,Rik ter Horst*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Proto-NUX原型机在法国比利牛斯山皮克杜米迪天文台的首次高海拔实地调试结果，验证了其作为地面近紫外（NUV）观测平台的可行性，但也揭示了光学性能未达预期、点扩散函数偏宽及探测器暗电流噪声显著等问题，并提出了后续改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展地面近紫外（300–350 nm）天文观测能力，弥补当前该波段观测设施的不足，支持对快速、高温暂现源的高频率搜寻。

Method: 构建单台原型机Proto-NUX（基于改装的36 cm RASA望远镜、定制NUV光学系统和UV敏感CMOS探测器），在高海拔（2877 m）天文台开展实地调试，全面测试部署、远程运行、定标、探测器性能、像质、跟踪、鬼像及同步光学监测等环节。

Result: 证实Proto-NUX可成功运行并满足主要技术要求，但实际NUV点扩散函数宽于预期，原因包括残余准直误差、离焦、色差和内部反射；探测器暗电流构成显著噪声源。

Conclusion: Proto-NUX是可行的地面NUV观测平台，但需优化光学准直与聚焦、抑制杂散光、降低探测器暗电流，相关改进对后续原型升级及全规模NUX设施建设至关重要。

Abstract: The Near-Ultraviolet eXplorer (NUX) is a proposed ground-based observing facility designed to expand near-ultraviolet (NUV) astronomy by enabling observations in the 300--350 nm range from Earth. The full system is intended to consist of four modified 36 cm Celestron RASA telescopes with custom NUV-optimized optics and UV-sensitive CMOS detectors, providing a total field of view of about 67 square degrees for high-cadence searches for fast, hot transients. To validate this concept, a single-telescope prototype, Proto-NUX, was constructed. Its optical design and development are described in previous work. Initial sea-level tests in the Netherlands showed that the instrument could be operated successfully and that the main technical requirements were met. In March 2026, Proto-NUX was deployed at the Pic du Midi Observatory in the French Pyrenees, at an altitude of 2877 m, for its first high-altitude on-sky commissioning campaign. The campaign tested the full system under realistic observing conditions, including deployment, remote operation, calibration procedures, detector behaviour, image quality, tracking, ghosting, and simultaneous optical monitoring. The observations demonstrate that Proto-NUX is a viable platform for ground-based NUV observations, but also show that the system was not yet operating at its expected optical limit. In particular, the delivered NUV point-spread function was broader than expected, with likely contributions from residual collimation error, defocus, chromatic effects, and internal reflections. The detector dark current was also found to be a significant contributor to the noise budget. We discuss these limitations and identify the main improvements needed for future Proto-NUX campaigns and for scaling the concept to the full NUX facility.

</details>


### [122] [An End-to-End Numerical Framework for Tidal Disruption Events with AthenaK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37859)
*Hong-Xuan Jiang,Mengqi Yang,David A. Velasco-Romero,Fangyuan Yu,Jing-Ze Xia,Xinyu Li,Yosuke Mizuno*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU加速有限体积代码AthenaK的新框架，用于完整模拟潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）全过程，涵盖恒星撕裂、碎片回落、吸积盘形成，并首次在单次模拟中全程包含自引力；通过多项关键算法改进（如移动参考系、自适应时间步、多网格泊松求解器等），显著提升精度与效率，并经严格验证和典型算例演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TDE模拟常将恒星撕裂、碎片动力学与吸积盘形成分阶段处理，忽略自引力等关键物理过程，且受限于计算效率与数值耗散；本文旨在构建一个统一、高保真、高效可扩展的全阶段TDE模拟框架。

Method: 基于GPU加速的有限体积代码AthenaK，新增移动参考系、域间重映射重启、AMR上的多网格泊松引力求解器、含氢氦复合物态方程（含复合效应）、冷超音速碎片流双能量更新、带黑洞挖除的移动势场、局部自适应时间步（LAT）等模块，并对各模块及整体进行独立与联合验证。

Result: 引力求解器在16.3个动力学时标内保持Lane-Emden球径向密度误差仅2.0e-3；双能量更新将马赫数7.75e7熵波压力误差从15.7降至4.0e-12；β=1牛顿TDE模拟复现了预期的能量分布、约50%束缚/非束缚物质比例及晚期t^(-5/3)回落率；高分辨率模拟在近心点喷嘴处热能增益与垂向动能损失吻合在5%以内，而低分辨率模拟因数值耗散过热；两模拟在返回流增厚后加热结果差异<12%；结合多频LTE后处理生成合成图像与光度。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了首例全程含自引力、高分辨率、多物理耦合的单次TDE全阶段模拟，显著提升了对碎片热力学演化与辐射特征的预测能力，为解释观测TDE光变与光谱提供了可靠数值基础。

Abstract: In a tidal disruption event (TDE), a star is torn apart by the tidal field of a black hole (BH), and its bound debris returns over many orbits to form an accretion disk. We present a framework built on the GPU-accelerated finite-volume code AthenaK that follows these stages in a single simulation with self-gravity throughout. We extend AthenaK with a moving simulation frame, restart remapping between domains, a multigrid Poisson solver on the adaptive mesh, a tabulated hydrogen-helium equation of state including recombination, a dual-energy update for the cold supersonic debris stream, a moving BH potential with excision, and localized adaptive time stepping (LAT), in which each MeshBlock advances on its own timestep, speeding up the production calculation by more than a factor of three. Each component is validated separately and in combination. The gravity solver maintains an isolated Lane-Emden sphere to a radial density error of 2.0e-3 over 16.3 dynamical times, and dual-energy recovery reduces the pressure error of a Mach 7.75e7 entropy wave from 15.7 to 4.0e-12. We demonstrate the framework with a Newtonian beta=1 disruption of a 1 Msun, 1 Rsun star by a 10^3 Msun BH. The debris has the expected energy spread, which is insensitive to the self-gravity update interval, splits evenly into bound and unbound material, and yields a fallback rate that approaches t^(-5/3) at late times. At the pericenter nozzle, the thermal energy gained in the high-resolution run matches the vertical kinetic energy lost to within 5%, whereas the fiducial run (4x lower resolution in x,y and 8x in z) suffers from excessive numerical dissipation that overheats the thinnest early stream. The heating in the two runs agrees to 12% once the returning stream has thickened. Multifrequency LTE post-processing turns the snapshots into synthetic images and luminosities.

</details>

### [123] [LIMFAST. V. Probing Reionization with the Synergy Between 21 cm and Photometric Galaxy Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35957)
*Guochao Sun,Adam Lidz,Tzu-Ching Chang,Claude-André Faucher-Giguère,Steven R. Furlanetto,Aritra Kundu,Jordan Mirocha*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用21cm信号平方与光度红移精度较低的莱曼断裂星系（LBGs）进行交叉相关的新方法，以克服传统交叉相关因红移精度不足而受限的问题，从而在再电离时期（EoR）物理参数约束上取得高显著性探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统21cm与星系交叉相关需要极高红移精度，排除了大量光度红移误差较大（σ_z ≳ 0.1）的LBG样本；亟需一种能兼容低精度红移星系的新统计量。

Method: 引入21cm信号平方（21cm²）作为新探针，通过平方操作将高k_∥模式耦合至低k_∥，使其可与光度红移星系交叉；结合Fisher矩阵分析与LIMFAST模拟，在z≈6–10开展LBG–21cm²交叉相关建模。

Result: 在SKA-Low（1000小时、15 MHz带宽）与Roman HLWAS巡天（m_AB,lim=27，σ_z≈0.1–0.3，10 deg²重叠区）联合下，LBG–21cm²交叉相关可在EoR大部分红移区间以高显著性探测；其大尺度交叉功率谱的符号演化对中性氢分数及IGM热/电离历史提供强约束。

Conclusion: LBG–21cm²交叉相关是一种可行且有力的新工具，可释放光度星系样本在EoR研究中的潜力，显著拓展未来SKA-Low与Roman协同科学的物理约束能力。

Abstract: Cross-correlating the 21\,cm signal from intergalactic neutral hydrogen with the galaxy distribution is a powerful probe of the Epoch of Reionization and the physical mechanisms driving it. While upcoming surveys with the SKA-Low radio telescope and the Roman Space Telescope offer ideal synergies for this measurement, foreground mitigation of 21\,cm data requires exquisite redshift precision of galaxies, effectively excluding the rich photometrically selected samples of Lyman-break galaxies (LBGs) with typical redshift uncertainties $σ_z \gtrsim 0.1$. We overcome this challenge using a squared statistic of the foreground-cleaned 21\,cm signal. Squaring the filtered 21\,cm signal couples surviving high $k_\parallel$ modes into low $k_\parallel$ fluctuations accessible to photometric LBG surveys. This higher-order cross-correlation statistic remains measurable even when direct cross-correlations are strongly suppressed. Applying Fisher matrix analysis to this LBG--21\,cm$^2$ cross-correlation signal simulated by the \texttt{LIMFAST} code over $6 \lesssim z \lesssim 10$, we investigate the prospects for constraining EoR physics combining SKA-Low 21\,cm measurements and Roman photometric galaxy samples. For an overlapping survey area covering 10\,deg$^2$, we find that the LBG--21\,cm$^2$ cross-correlation is detectable at high significance across much of the EoR through 1000\,hours of SKA-Low integration with 15\,MHz bandwidth and a Roman photometric galaxy sample from the High-Latitude Wide-Area Survey (HLWAS) reaching $m_\mathrm{AB,lim}=27$ and photo-$z$ uncertainties of $σ_z \sim 0.1$--0.3. The characteristic sign evolution of the large-scale cross-power spectrum---driven primarily, though not exclusively, by the evolving neutral fraction---provides strong constraints on the physical parameters governing the ionization and thermal history of the IGM.

</details>


### [124] [Current and Future Constraints on the Primordial Power Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35964)
*Zachary Cheslog,Emily Finson,Amanda MacInnis,Neelima Sehgal,Niayesh Afshordi,Simran K. Nerval,Renée Hložek*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了早期宇宙物理模型（如Starobinsky暴胀和Bi-thermal大爆炸模型）在扩展ΛCDM模型（含ns、αs、Neff、∑mν）下的兼容性，发现CMB与DESI数据联合分析使这些模型不再被排除，并展望了未来CMB实验对原初功率谱的更高精度约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验早期宇宙模型（如暴胀模型）在更广义宇宙学参数空间中的可行性，尤其是当允许Neff和中微子质量自由变化时，是否仍能与观测数据兼容。

Method: 基于Planck、ACT、SPT（CMB-PAS）与DESI DR2联合数据，在九参数ΛCDM+αs+Neff+∑mν模型下进行贝叶斯参数估计与误差分析，并对未来CMB实验（如SO、CMB-HD）开展预测性Fisher矩阵分析。

Result: 在九参数模型下，ns=0.9758±0.0064，αs=0.0080±0.0064，Starobinsky暴胀与Bi-thermal模型均与数据一致；CMB-HD有望将原初功率谱在k=0.2 Mpc⁻¹处的约束提升至0.1%，并在k=30 Mpc⁻¹处实现首次测量。

Conclusion: 放宽Neff和∑mν可显著缓解ns参数限制，使若干早期宇宙模型重获生机；未来高精度CMB实验将极大提升对原初功率谱形状的刻画能力，为甄别暴胀机制提供关键依据。

Abstract: The primordial scalar power spectrum provides a powerful window onto early-universe physics, including a broad class of inflationary scenarios. These models often predict a characteristic scalar spectral index $(n_s)$, nonzero running $(α_s)$, or more general deviations from a pure power-law form. We find that freeing the number of light relic species $(N_{\rm eff})$ and the sum of the neutrino masses $(\sum m_ν)$ broadens the $n_s$ error contours enough that key early-Universe models are no longer excluded. In particular, while both Starobinsky inflation and the Bi-thermal Big Bang model are ruled out at about the 95% confidence level in the six-parameter $Λ$CDM model, we find that in the nine-parameter $Λ{\rm CDM} + α_s + N_{\rm eff} + \sum m_ν$ model, CMB data from Planck, ACT, and SPT (CMB-PAS) combined with DESI DR2 give $n_s = 0.9758 \pm 0.0064$ and $α_s = 0.0080 \pm 0.0064$, consistent with both models. Future CMB facilities will sharply improve constraints on $n_s$, $α_s$, and, more generally, the binned primordial power spectrum $\mathcal{P}(k)$. For example, CMB-PAS constrains $e^{-2τ}\mathcal{P}(k = 0.2\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1})$ to 0.5%, while SO-like and CMB-HD-like surveys would tighten this to 0.1%; CMB-HD would also extend this measurement to smaller, previously unprobed scales, constraining $e^{-2τ}\mathcal{P}(k = 30\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1})$ to within a factor of ten. We release our forecast code and update the public CMB-HD likelihood and Fisher codes to support CLASS in addition to CAMB.

</details>


### [125] [JWST lensed quasar dark matter survey V: Hints of self-interacting dark matter from 29 quadruply imaged quasars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35974)
*D. Gilman,A. M. Nierenberg,J. Gurian,H. Paugnat,C. Gannon,M. N. Martinez,T. Treu,K. N. Abazajian,T. Anguita,V. N. Bennert,A. J. Benson,S. Birrer,S. G. Djorgovski,S. F. Hoenig,R. E. Keeley,A. Kusenko,M. Millon,T. Morishita,L. A. Moustakas,P. Mozumdar,D. Paris,W. Sheu,D. Sluse,K. C. Wong*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用29个四重成像类星体，通过低质量扰动体的集体引力透镜效应，在10^6–10^10.7 M⊙质量范围内对自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）模型预言的核心坍缩暗晕（含子晕）丰度进行群体级推断；结果强烈支持核心坍缩存在，贝叶斯因子显示SIDM显著优于冷暗物质（CDM）模型（6:1至24:1），对CDM范式构成挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）理论中暗晕核心坍缩的可观测后果，并挑战标准冷暗物质（CDM）模型在小尺度结构上的预测。

Method: 基于29个四重成像类星体的强引力透镜观测数据，采用群体级贝叶斯推断方法，建模结构形成过程中核心坍缩（子）晕的红移与质量依赖丰度，并系统评估球状星团丰度、子晕丰度及坍缩晕内禀结构等系统误差的影响。

Result: 观测数据强烈偏好包含核心坍缩（子）晕的模型；贝叶斯因子显示SIDM相对于CDM的置信度为6:1至24:1；若坚持CDM则需引入远超预期的球状星团数量和多于N体模拟预测的子晕数量。

Conclusion: 结果为SIDM提供了强有力观测支持，对CDM在亚星系尺度上的有效性构成严峻挑战；核心坍缩暗晕是解释当前强透镜观测的关键成分。

Abstract: Theories of self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) predict the eventual core collapse of dark matter halos, a process that transforms these structures into extremely efficient gravitational lenses. We present a population-level inference on the abundance of core collapsed halos and subhalos in the mass range $10^6 - 10^{10.7} M_{\odot}$ using 29 quadruply imaged quasars, drawing on the collective lensing signal of many low-mass perturbers. Our structure formation model predicts the abundance of collapsed (sub)halos as a function of halo mass and redshift. We quantify how our results are affected by systematic uncertainties, including the abundance of globular clusters (GCs) and subhalos, and the internal structure of deeply collapsed halos. Our data exhibits a strong preference for core collapsed halos in the lens model, with Bayes factors disfavoring CDM relative to SIDM ranging from 6:1 to 24:1, depending on the assumed properties of the SIDM subhalo and GC populations. Explaining our data without core collapse requires both a GC abundance well above expectations and more subhalos than predicted by $N$-body simulations. These results pose a challenge to the CDM paradigm. We present a particle physics interpretation of these results in a companion paper.

</details>


### [126] [Satellite stellar-to-halo mass relations in primordial black hole universes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35988)
*Patricio Colazo,Nelson Padilla,Federico Stasyszyn*

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定共形时间（FCT）扩展质量函数的原初黑洞（PBHs）对小尺度结构的影响，特别是对卫星星系-暗晕映射、束缚残余致密性及致密暗扰动体关联的影响；通过对比CDM与FCT/PBH数值模拟，发现FCT增强子晕丰度并提高其致密性，但基于丰度的恒星质量重分配削弱了卫星恒星质量函数的区分能力，而内部结构（如致密性、NFW拟合质量、形成红移）更具诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究原初黑洞（尤其是具有固定共形时间质量函数的PBHs）如何通过增强原初功率谱和泊松离散涨落来改变小尺度结构，进而影响卫星星系分布、子晕致密性及其与致密暗扰动体的联系。

Method: 采用两个纯暗物质数值模拟（标准CDM与含FCT/PBH模型），因模拟中未生成星系，构建基于丰度匹配的近似恒星-晕映射（使用最后孤立时的前体维里质量）；利用解析的SOAP参数（V_max, R_max）表征当前子晕致密性，并加入未解析PBH种群的质量分异亚网格估计。

Result: FCT显著增强质量依赖的子晕丰度，但丰度驱动的恒星质量重分配使卫星恒星质量函数难以区分模型；FCT子晕更致密、NFW重构质量更大、形成更早，导致类似‘太大而不能失败’的张力；PBH质量分异引入中心扰动（μ~0.4–0.5），但无法完全解释观测到的致密性增强；卫星数密度易被映射变化掩盖，而动力学特征更具判别力。

Conclusion: 卫星丰度、内部动力学与致密暗扰动体是互补探针，共同约束PBH对小尺度结构的物理影响；其中子晕内部结构（致密性、形成历史、质量分布）比丰度统计更敏感、更稳健。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs), in particular with a fixed-conformal-time (FCT) extended mass function, can modify small-scale structure through enhanced primordial power and discreteness-driven Poisson fluctuations, altering subhalo abundances and internal structure. We study their effects on satellite stellar-to-halo mappings, bound-remnant compactness, and the link to compact dark perturbers. We compare two dark-matter-only simulations, a fiducial CDM run and an FCT/PBH run. As these runs form no galaxies, we construct an approximate abundance-based stellar-to-halo mapping using the progenitor virial mass at last isolation. The CDM mapping is anchored to a redshift-dependent abundance-matching stellar-to-halo relation, and the FCT mapping is derived from the relative cumulative subhalo abundances. We use resolved SOAP (V_max) and (R_max) to characterize present-day subhalo compactness, supplemented by a subgrid estimate of mass segregation in the unresolved PBH population. FCT enhances the subhalo abundance in a mass-dependent way, but abundance-based stellar-mass reassignment makes satellite stellar-mass functions weak discriminants. Internal structure is a stronger test: FCT subhaloes are more compact and have larger NFW-reconstructed enclosed masses at fixed radius, strengthening a Too-Big-To-Fail-like tension if all compact subhaloes host luminous satellites. FCT subhaloes also form earlier, accounting for much of the low-mass compactness difference, while PBH mass segregation adds a central perturbation with typical μ~ 0.4-0.5. Neither effect fully explains the resolved FCT compactness enhancement. Changes in the galaxy--halo mapping can partly hide the enhanced FCT subhalo abundance in satellite counts, whereas internal dynamics remain more discriminating. Satellite abundances, dynamics, and compact dark perturbers are complementary probes of PBH-induced small-scale structure.

</details>


### [127] [Enhanced density fluctuations: consequences for stellar dynamics and dark matter substructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35991)
*Wenzer Qin,David E. Kaplan,Neal Weiner*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了早期暗物质晕中增强的密度涨落如何影响高红移下恒星、黑洞及星团的形成，并分析了不同原初功率谱增强模型对银河系捕获星团数量及暗物质子晕丰度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释‘小红点’天体丰度观测，并探究增强密度涨落对早期结构形成（如无星暗晕、早期星系、星团）的影响机制。

Method: 采用简化模型增强原初功率谱，结合标准天体物理参数，计算不同增强形状（尖峰型 vs 阶跃型）下早期星团数量、初始恒星质量及暗物质子晕丰度的变化。

Result: 尖峰型增强产生约50个星团，阶跃型增强达~4.1×10⁵个；星团初始恒星质量约O(100) M⊙；质量>10⁷ M⊙的暗物质子晕丰度最多增强6.5倍且更致密，提升其在Via等恒星流巡天中的可探测性。

Conclusion: 原初功率谱的局部形状显著调控早期致密结构的形成效率与可观测性，为检验早期宇宙物理及暗物质性质提供新探针。

Abstract: Enhanced density fluctuations form early dark matter halos, which serve as early gravitational potential wells for baryons. These baryons can then condense to form stars and black holes at high redshifts, $1+z \gtrsim 100$. Larger density fluctuations result in denser halos with higher virial temperatures; hence, a halo at a mass that is below the threshold for forming stars under standard astrophysical expectations may form astrophysical objects if its initial density is higher than expected, although the exact threshold for compact object formation is subject to large astrophysical uncertainties. We outline the conditions under which enhanced density fluctuations form dark (star-less) halos, early galaxies, or even star clusters. Using toy model enhancements to the primordial power spectrum that have been shown to explain the abundances of ``little red dots", along with our fiducial set of astrophysical parameters, we show that the number of clusters initially captured by the Milky Way is highly sensitive to the shape of the enhancement, with the narrowly peaked enhancement resulting in $50$ clusters and the step-function enhancement resulting in $\sim 4.1 \times 10^5$. The initial stellar masses of the clusters is also $O (100) \,M_\odot$, although this range is also dependent on the shape of the enhancement. In addition, the abundance of dark matter subhalos with masses greater than $10^7 \, M_\odot$ can be enhanced by as much as a factor of $\sim 6.5$ and is denser than in $Λ$CDM, which will increase their detectability in future stellar stream surveys such as the Via Project.

</details>


### [128] [Cosmological-perturbation equations from the total-angular-momentum formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36000)
*Marc Kamionkowski,Robert R. Caldwell*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用总角动量（TAM）波推导宇宙学扰动的线性演化方程，得到CMB温度与偏振的传递函数积分形式，并由此导出标量和张量扰动的标准玻尔兹曼层次微分方程；同时探讨了各向同性与各向异性宇宙双折射效应及其对温度涨落的微妙影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提供物理图像更清晰、更具洞察力的宇宙学扰动演化方程推导方法，并系统研究宇宙双折射（尤其是其各向异性）对CMB观测的影响。

Method: 基于总角动量（TAM）波框架，推导CMB温度和偏振转移函数的积分方程，并从中导出标量与张量扰动的玻尔兹曼层次微分方程；纳入各向同性及各向异性宇宙双折射效应进行分析。

Result: 成功从TAM波出发导出了CMB转移函数积分方程及对应的玻尔兹曼方程；揭示了宇宙双折射（特别是各向异性情形）对CMB温度涨落存在微妙但重要的影响。

Conclusion: TAM波方法不仅可复现标准结果，还提供了更深刻的物理理解；宇宙双折射效应，尤其是其各向异性成分，值得在CMB数据分析中进一步关注。

Abstract: We derive the equations for the linear evolution of cosmological perturbations using total-angular-momentum (TAM) waves. The cosmic microwave background temperature and polarization transfer functions are first derived as integral equations and from these the usual Boltzmann hierarchy of differential equations are then obtained for both scalar and tensor perturbations. We include the effects of isotropic cosmic birefringence and discuss anisotropic cosmic birefringence. We note a subtle effect of cosmic birefringence on temperature fluctuations. The TAM derivation of the equations provides physical insights to results obtained previously.

</details>


### [129] [Connecting the Dots: Prospects for Detecting Multi-Source Inflation through Scale-Dependent and Stochastic Bias of Little Red Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36002)
*Peter L. Taylor,Xinyi Chen,Christopher M. Hirata,Charuhas Shiveshwarkar*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用小红点（LRDs）的空间聚类作为高红移宇宙学探针的可行性，并预测其对原初局域型非高斯性参数 $f^{m loc}_{m NL}$ 和塌缩极限四谱振幅 $τ^{m loc}_{m NL}$ 的约束能力，结果表明LRD分析在探测多源暴胀方面具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用LRDs大体积覆盖、高偏置和易识别的特性，提升对原初非高斯性参数 $f^{m loc}_{m NL}$ 和 $τ^{m loc}_{m NL}$ 的观测约束能力，进而检验多源暴胀模型。

Method: 基于理想化联合 Fisher 预测框架，假设普适性关系、高斯协方差、泊松散粒噪声及归一化红移弥散 $σ(z)/(1+z)=0.03$，量化在 Suyama-Yamaguchi 关系 $τ^{m loc}_{m NL} > (6/5 f_{m NL})^2$ 下探测多源暴胀的显著性。

Result: LRD 分析可在参数空间大片区域内以高显著性稳健探测多源暴胀，其约束能力优于所有已开展或计划中的其他实验。

Conclusion: LRDs 是极具前景的高红移宇宙学探针，有望在原初非高斯性研究中实现突破性进展。

Abstract: Recent work has proposed using the spatial clustering of Little Red Dots (LRDs) as a high-redshift cosmological probe. In this paper, we investigate the feasibility of obtaining a LRD sample with a future space experiment, and forecast LRD constraints on primordial local-type non-Gaussianity, $f^{\rm loc}_{\rm NL}$, and the collapsed-limit trispectrum amplitude, $τ^{\rm loc}_{\rm NL}$. LRDs are ideally suited to probe these inflationary parameters because they span a large cosmological volume, have high bias and are easily identifiable. We perform idealised joint $f_{\rm NL}^{\rm loc}$--$τ_{\rm NL}^{\rm loc}$ Fisher forecasts assuming the universality relation, Gaussian covariance, Poisson shot-noise and a normalised redshift dispersion, $σ(z) / \left( {1+z} \right)= 0.03$. We quantify the significance at which one would detect multi-source inflation under the Suyama--Yamaguchi relation, $τ^{\rm loc}_{\rm NL} > \left( 6/5 f_{\rm NL}\right)^2$, across the $f_{\rm NL}^{\rm loc}$--$τ_{\rm NL}^{\rm loc}$ plane. We find that a LRD analysis could robustly detect multi-source inflation at high-significance over a large region of parameter space, and would be more constraining than all other past or present experiments.

</details>


### [130] [Constraining Reionization with Persistent Homology: a Novel Summary Statistic for Wide-Field Lyman-$α$ Emitter Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36092)
*Michael M. Wyatt,Nikolaos Triantafyllou,Steven R. Furlanetto,Hiroya Umeda,Samuel Gagnon-Hartman,Andrei Mesinger,Masami Ouchi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入持续同调（persistent homology）中的Betti-0曲线作为表征莱曼α发射体（LAEs）空间聚类的新统计量，并证明其在约束再电离时期中性氢分数x_HI方面性能优于或至少不逊于传统角相关函数（ACF）和空洞概率函数（VPF），三者联合使用可将约束精度提升约2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用LAEs的空间聚类约束宇宙再电离过程，但传统统计量（如ACF、VPF）可能无法充分捕捉拓扑结构信息，需探索更有效的摘要统计量。

Method: 采用持续同调分析中的Betti-0曲线作为新摘要统计量；基于21cmFAST模拟LAES观测，结合SILVERRUSH巡天数据（z=5.7和6.6），通过Fisher信息量化各统计量对中性氢分数x_HI的约束能力，并考虑宇宙方差不确定性。

Result: Betti-0曲线在约束x_HI方面表现与ACF和VPF相当或更优；三者敏感尺度互补，联合使用使约束精度提高约2倍，最小不确定性达σ_min ~ 0.03（z=5.7, x_HI=0.05）和0.07（z=6.6, x_HI=0.20）。

Conclusion: Betti-0曲线是约束再电离的有效新工具，持续同调方法值得在Roman望远镜等宽视场巡天中进一步应用。

Abstract: The spatial clustering of Lyman-$α$ emitters (LAEs) is one of the most promising observables for constraining reionization. In this work, we introduce persistent homology, a popular tool in topological data analysis, and specifically the Betti-0 curve as a summary statistic to characterize the clustering of LAEs. We compare the performance of the Betti-0 curve against the angular correlation function (ACF) and void probability function (VPF) by quantifying their ability to constrain the global neutral fraction $x_{\rm HI}$ during reionization using the Fisher information. We forward model observations of LAEs using 21cmFAST by including $\mathrm{Ly}α$ emission as a post-processing step and account for cosmic variance uncertainties. We focus our analysis within the framework of the SILVERRUSH. catalog at $z=5.7$ and 6.6, and find that the Betti-0 curve performs as well as or better than the ACF and VPF. We also find that the statistics are most sensitive to different physical scales and thus probe complementary information, and as a consequence that simultaneously constraining all three statistics improves constraints by a factor of $\sim 2$ compared with the ACF alone, with predicted minimum uncertainties of $σ_{\rm min} \sim$ 0.03 and 0.07 at $x_{\rm HI} = 0.05$ and $z=5.7$ and $x_{\rm HI} = 0.20$ and $z=6.6$, respectively. Our results show that the Betti-0 curve is a promising summary statistic to constrain reionization using LAE clustering and motivate further exploration of persistent homology for the same purpose in other wide-field surveys such as those made possible by the Nancy Grace Roman Telescope.

</details>


### [131] [Probing Baryons with the Kinematic Sunyae Zel'dovich Effect and Machine Learning Derived Peculiar Velocities using DESI DR2 and ACT DR6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36355)
*Yulin Gong,Rachel Bean,Patricio A. Gallardo,Boryana Hadzhiyska,Yun-Hsin Hsu,Jenna Moore,Eve M. Vavagiakis,Nicholas Battaglia,J. Aguilar,S. Ahlen,A. Aviles,F. Beutler,D. Bianchi,D. Brooks,A. Carnero Rosell,T. Claybaugh,A. de la Macorra,Arjun Dey,Biprateep Dey,P. Doel,A. Font-Ribera,J. E. Forero-Romero,E. Gaztañaga,G. Gutierrez,K. Honscheid,S. Juneau,T. Karim,R. Kehoe,D. Kirkby,A. Kremin,O. Lahav,M. Landriau,L. Le Guillou,M. Manera,A. Meisner,R. Miquel,S. Nadathur,J. A. Newman,H. E. Noriega,E. Paillas,N. Palanque-Delabrouille,W. J. Percival,F. Prada,I. Pérez-Ràfols,C. Ravoux,G. Rossi,L. Samushia,E. Sanchez,C. Saulder,D. Schlegel,M. Schubnell,H. Seo,J. Silber,M. Siudek,G. Tarlé,B. A. Weaver,R. Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文结合kSZ效应测量与机器学习重建的非线性本动速度，对DESI DR2亮红星系（LRG）所追踪的大质量晕的平均光学深度和气体密度分布给出了新的观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测弥漫重子物质和大尺度相干速度场，弥补传统线性方法的不足，并充分利用新一代星系巡天与CMB数据。

Method: 利用ACT DR6 CMB温度图（ILC和150 GHz）测量LRG样本的成对kSZ信号；采用基于模拟训练的Transformer模型，在z=0.55和0.8两个红移处重建LRG视线方向本动速度；通过比较kSZ与速度相关函数推断平均光学深度，并获取速度加权AP滤波的kSZ堆叠密度剖面。

Result: 在不同LRG亮度样本中均获得显著kSZ探测（最高信噪比达14.2和15.1）；发现大质量LRG集团周围存在延展电离气体证据；当前误差范围内未见明显红移演化。

Conclusion: kSZ与机器学习重建速度的联合分析是探测弥散重子和大尺度速度场的有效新途径，为Euclid、Roman、Rubin LSST等未来巡天与CCAT、Simons Observatory等CMB实验提供了重要方法学基础。

Abstract: We present novel empirical constraints on average optical depths and gas density profiles from combining kinematic Sunyaev Zel'dovich effect (kSZ) measurements and nonlinear peculiar velocities, reconstructed using machine learning, for massive halos traced by DESI DR2 luminous red galaxies (LRG). The pairwise kSZ is measured using two ACT DR6 cosmic microwave background (CMB) temperature maps (ILC and 150 GHz maps) for seven luminosity-selected LRG samples. We find consistent results between the two maps and significant detections across the samples. We reconstruct LRG line-of-sight peculiar velocities in two cosmic epochs, centered on $z=$ 0.55 and 0.8, using a simulation-trained Transformer machine learning model to extend beyond the linear approximation. Average optical depths are inferred by comparing the kSZ pairwise correlation to the measured pairwise velocity correlation from the reconstructed velocity field and, separately, from a theoretical prediction using the best fit Planck cosmology, with the highest significance reaching SNR = 14.2 and 15.1 respectively. We also obtain velocity-weighted AP-filtered kSZ-stacked cluster density profiles, which show evidence of extended ionized gas around massive LRG groups and no strong redshift evolution within current measurement uncertainties. These results demonstrate the power of combining spectroscopic galaxy and CMB data, and employing machine learning methods, to probe diffuse baryons and coherent large-scale velocity fields. They open up new avenues to leverage upcoming galaxy survey data from Euclid, Roman and Rubin LSST, in tandem with multi-frequency CMB/sub-mm data from CCAT and Simons Observatory.

</details>


### [132] [Everything Everywhere All At Once: A Hierarchical Framework for Mapping Anisotropies in the Local Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36462)
*Aryan Bansal,Edward N. Taylor,Michelle Cluver,Matthew Colless*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯分层前向模型，用于从异质红移巡天数据中重建局域光度-密度场，成功应用于2MRS、6dFGS和GAMA巡天，验证了局部宇宙在大尺度上与ΛCDM预期一致，仅在最近红移壳层（z≈0.01）存在显著欠密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红移巡天数据具有异质性（不同选择函数、深度和天区覆盖），需一种能统一处理多源数据并保留各自选择效应的方法，以高保真重建局部光度-密度场并检验宇宙学模型。

Method: 构建贝叶斯分层前向模型：将巡天体积划分为角单元与红移壳层，在视星等-红移空间直接建模观测星系分布；各单元独立拟合光度函数与密度，同时通过层级结构联合约束宇宙平均光度函数、其红移演化及单元间变异超参数。

Result: 在z<0.065范围内联合分析2MRS、6dFGS和GAMA数据，成功恢复J波段光度函数全局参数和平均光度密度；发现光度-密度散射随体积增大而减小，符合ΛCDM宇宙学预期的宇宙方差；最近红移壳层（z≈0.01）存在2.6σ（单边显著性≈99.5%）的欠密度（约25%）。

Conclusion: 局部宇宙整体与平滑演化加宇宙方差预期一致，未发现大尺度相干欠密度；该模型为下一代宽深巡天提供了可扩展的密度场重建框架。

Abstract: We present a Bayesian hierarchical forward model for mapping the local luminosity-density field from heterogeneous redshift surveys. By dividing the survey volume into angular cells and redshift shells, we model the observed galaxy distribution directly in apparent-magnitude and redshift space. This allows us to infer cell-level luminosity functions and densities while preserving each survey's unique selection function. Simultaneously, the ensemble of cells constrains the cosmic-mean luminosity function, its redshift evolution, and the hyperparameters describing cell-to-cell variation. We apply the framework to 2MRS, 6dFGS, and GAMA over $0.005<z<0.065$, combining wide sky coverage with deeper and fainter galaxy samples. The model successfully recovers global $J$-band luminosity-function parameters and a mean luminosity density consistent with previous low-redshift measurements. The inferred luminosity-density scatter decreases with volume, consistent with the cosmic-variance amplitude expected in a $Λ$CDM universe. Within the volume we probe, we find no evidence for a large coherent underdensity. Instead, the local Universe is broadly consistent with smooth evolution and cosmic variance. The nearest shell at $z \simeq 0.01$ is the clearest exception, lying $\simeq 0.12$~dex ($\simeq 25\%$) below the smooth global model, a $-2.6σ$ underdensity with a one-sided significance of $\simeq 99.5\%$. This framework offers a scalable architecture for mapping cosmic density fields with next-generation wide and deep surveys.

</details>


### [133] [Cross-correlating Squared kSZ and HI Intensity Fields: A Map-level ACT--MeerKAT Forecast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36863)
*Yu-Er Jiang,Yun Liu,Yan Gong,Zi-Yan Yuwen,Yin-Zhe Ma,Furen Deng,Xuelei Chen,Qi Guo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了平方kSZ效应与平方HI强度场之间的交叉相关性，利用Jiutian-1G模拟构建了ACT和MeerKAT的模拟观测数据，结果表明该交叉相关信号在未来的联合观测中具有可探测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kSZ效应因其黑体谱特性和速度依赖符号难以分离，且与密度示踪剂的传统互相关为零，亟需新方法提取其信号。

Method: 基于Jiutian-1G模拟光锥生成halo-based kSZ天图和MeerKAT类HI亮度温度立方体，并加入真实仪器效应（如ACT光束平滑、噪声；MeerKAT频率相关光束、扫描策略、成图及非均匀热噪声），分析信号仅存、理想振幅重建及Wiener滤波三种kSZ图的平方场交叉相关。

Result: kSZ² × HI²交叉相关在模拟中获得约3–4的累积信噪比，证实其在联合ACT–MeerKAT观测中具备可探测性。

Conclusion: 平方场交叉相关是一种可行的新探针，可用于未来CMB与21cm巡天联合分析中提取kSZ信号。

Abstract: The kinetic Sunyaev--Zel'dovich (kSZ) effect probes the line-of-sight momentum of free electrons, but its blackbody spectrum and velocity-dependent sign make it difficult to isolate and cause conventional cross-correlations with density tracers to vanish. In this work, we investigate the cross-correlation of the squared kSZ field with the projected squared HI intensity field using mock ACT and MeerKAT observations constructed from a Jiutian-1G simulation lightcone. We generate halo-based kSZ maps and MeerKAT-like HI brightness-temperature cubes over a $13.5^\circ\times13.5^\circ$ field. The simulations incorporate ACT beam smoothing and effective internal-linear-combination noise, together with the frequency-dependent MeerKAT beam, scanning strategy, map-making, and inhomogeneous thermal noise. We analyze signal-only, idealized amplitude-reconstructed, and Wiener-filtered kSZ maps. The resulting squared-field cross-correlations have diagnostic cumulative signal-to-noise ratios of approximately $3$-$4$. Our map-level results demonstrate the detectability of the ${\rm kSZ}^2\times {\rm HI}^2$ correlation in future joint ACT--MeerKAT observations.

</details>


### [134] [Weak Lensing Measurements from the Lyman-$α$ Forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36949)
*Anže Slosar*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI DR1数据，通过构建Lyman-α森林的二次弱引力透镜估计量，并将其投影到由DESI和BOSS低红移星系与类星体构建的偏转模板上，首次尝试测量Lyman-α森林的弱透镜效应；结果表明当前数据信噪比不足，未能明确探测该效应，测得的透镜振幅与无透镜或标准宇宙学预测（AL=1）均一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量Lyman-α森林的弱引力透镜效应，以检验其作为宇宙大尺度结构探针的潜力，并为未来巡天提供方法学验证。

Method: 基于DESI DR1的Lyman-α森林和类星体数据，构建森林-森林及类星体-森林二次相关性估计量；使用DESI/BOSS低红移星系与类星体构建引力偏转模板；通过与ACT和Planck CMB透镜图比对验证模板，并用注入位移测试估计器可靠性。

Result: 森林自相关给出AL = 0.42 ± 0.43，类星体–森林交叉相关给出AL = 0.87 ± 0.48，联合结果为AL = 0.62 ± 0.33，与AL = 1（标准理论）及AL = 0（无透镜）均在1σ内一致。

Conclusion: 当前公开数据（DESI DR1等）信噪比不足以明确探测Lyman-α森林弱透镜信号；但所提方法已获验证，为后续更高精度数据（如DESI后续释放）开展该测量奠定基础。

Abstract: We attempt to measure the weak lensing of the Lyman-$α$ forest by projecting a quadratic estimator of the forest-forest and quasar-forest pair correlations, built from the DESI DR1 data, onto deflection templates constructed from low-redshift galaxies and quasars from DESI and BOSS. After validating deflection templates against the ACT DR6 and Planck PR4 CMB lensing maps and testing the estimator with injected displacements we measure the lensing amplitude $A_L$ on the data. Consistent with predictions in the literature, the current generation of public data does not have sufficient signal-to-noise to measure this effect unequivocally: we find the lensing amplitude $A_L=0.42\pm0.43$ from the forest auto-correlation and $0.87\pm0.48$ from the quasar--forest cross-correlation. These two nearly independent measurements combine to $A_L=0.62\pm0.33$, consistent with both the fiducial value $A_L=1$ and with no lensing.

</details>


### [135] [A halo-based intrinsic-alignment model for simulation-based inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37254)
*M. Gatti*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于晕（halo）的本征对齐（IA）模型，包含中心星系和卫星星系两个部分，并在FLAMINGO模拟中验证其对两体及高阶统计量（如IA、弱引力透镜、密度场及其交叉关联）的建模能力；相比传统NLA和δNLA模型，该晕模型显著提升了非线性尺度和高阶可观测量的拟合精度，且仅需少量参数（4参数可移植模型），并可在不同N体模拟间迁移，为基于模拟的弱透镜推断提供了更优的IA建模方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本征对齐（IA）模型（如NLA）在非线性尺度和高阶统计量上表现不佳，难以满足仿真驱动的弱透镜推断对真实IA场建模的高要求。

Method: 构建一种分离中心与卫星星系贡献的晕基IA模型；在FLAMINGO流体动力学模拟中，联合拟合IA、透镜和密度场的两体及高阶统计量（含星系间本征形变关联）；对比NLA、δNLA等模型；进一步通过简化的晕占据模型重建源星系样本，并测试模型在纯N体模拟中的可迁移性。

Result: 晕模型在原始星系位置上全面优于NLA/δNLA，能同时准确描述所有统计量；即使星系占据仅被近似重建，性能仍保持；四参数简化版具备良好可移植性，并成功迁移至独立N体模拟；模型不依赖大量烦琐参数。

Conclusion: 基于晕的IA模型显著提升非线性场级IA建模精度，参数简洁、可迁移性强，是面向仿真弱透镜推断的有前景IA建模路径，但尚需在更广质量范围、多种流体模拟及实际巡天选择下进一步验证。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference and other field-level weak-lensing analyses require intrinsic-alignment (IA) models generating realistic intrinsic-ellipticity fields across two-point and non-Gaussian observables. We develop a halo-based IA prescription with separate central and satellite components and test it against intrinsic galaxy shapes in FLAMINGO. We jointly fit two-point and higher-order statistics involving the IA, lensing, and density fields, including correlations between the intrinsic shapes of distinct galaxies, and compare against field-level nonlinear-alignment and density-weighted NLA prescriptions. We first evaluate the models at the positions of the original FLAMINGO galaxies, fixing the galaxy--matter connection and isolating the IA response. NLA and $δ$NLA leave substantial discrepancies on nonlinear scales and across higher-order observables, whereas the halo model provides a simultaneous description of all statistics. We then remove the FLAMINGO galaxy catalogue and regenerate the source population from the halo field using a low-dimensional halo occupation model; the agreement is preserved even when the detailed occupation is only approximately reproduced. Much of this performance can be retained with a compact four-parameter portable model, and the same architecture transfers successfully to an independent gravity-only N-body realization with different halo definitions and shape measurements. These results show that nonlinear field-level IA modelling can be substantially improved over simple NLA-like prescriptions without introducing a large nuisance-parameter space. The halo model therefore provides a promising route for incorporating intrinsic alignments into simulation-based weak-lensing inference, although further validation across halo masses, hydrodynamical simulations, and realistic survey selections will be needed before application to data.

</details>


### [136] [Carving out the Multifield Cosmological Collider Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37570)
*Denis Werth*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多场暴胀模型中宇宙学胶子信号的普遍性，发现当场数较大时（约100个），由于极值统计和随机矩阵普适性，几乎局域的双谱形状成为普遍预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由具体紫外嵌入所预言的多场暴胀模型中，宇宙学胶子信号是否具有普遍性尚不明确，本文旨在探究其在大场数极限下的行为特征。

Method: 构建曲率扰动与N_f个附加场之间最一般的破缺洛伦兹提升对称性的混合项，并提取决定宇宙学关联函数软极限行为的晚期标度维数。

Result: 当场数较少时，增强的挤压极限（可能伴随振荡）是一种微调信号；当场数较大（~100）时，混合项使标度维数普遍趋近于幺正边界，导致几乎局域的双谱形状成为普遍现象。

Conclusion: 多场暴胀中几乎局域的宇宙学胶子信号并非特例，而是大N_f下由极值统计和随机矩阵普适性所保证的通用结果。

Abstract: We show that inflation with a large number of fields, as predicted by concrete ultraviolet embeddings, generically produces almost-local cosmological collider signals in the bispectrum squeezed limit. We construct the most general boost-breaking mixings between the curvature perturbation and $N_{\rm f}$ additional fields, and extract the late-time scaling dimensions that dictate the soft limits of cosmological correlators. For a handful of fields, an enhanced squeezed limit (with potential oscillations) is a fine-tuned signal. At large $N_{\rm f}\sim100$, however, mixings generically drive the scaling dimensions toward the unitarity boundary. We show that this is a direct consequence of extreme value statistics and random-matrix universality, turning an almost-local bispectrum shape into a generic prediction for almost any masses and mixings.

</details>


### [137] [Gravitational-wave dark sirens as astrophysical probes: inferring galaxy-merger relations and identifying individual hosts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38110)
*Alessandro Agapito,Michele Mancarella*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的分层框架，将引力波‘暗警报’技术从宇宙学工具拓展为天体物理探针，同时推断致密双星在星系中的分布及其单个宿主星系身份，并成功在GW170817事件中识别出NGC 4993为最可能宿主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗警报技术主要用于宇宙学，但其蕴含的致密双星在星系中分布及具体宿主星系等关键天体物理信息尚未被利用。

Method: 构建一个统一的分层贝叶斯框架，联合推断致密双星的星系分布模型与单个引力波事件的宿主星系，生成概率排序的目标星系列表。

Result: 对GW170817盲分析成功将NGC 4993列为最高优先级宿主；同时约束了星系光度加权模型，轻微支持非均匀加权；固定加权则可能损害宿主识别精度。

Conclusion: 该框架使暗警报成为研究电磁沉默致密双星并合天体物理性质的独特探针，兼具宿主识别与分布建模能力。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) ``dark siren'' techniques, namely the statistical association of GW events with a galaxy catalog, have so far been used as a cosmological tool. Yet this association carries distinctive astrophysical information that remains unused---specifically, how compact binaries populate galaxies and the identities of individual hosts. In this work, we turn the dark-siren program into an astrophysical probe by inferring both simultaneously within a single hierarchical framework. The method reduces the many potential hosts to a compact, probabilistically ranked target list, providing a direct follow-up decision tool. Applied blindly to GW170817, it recovers NGC~4993 as the highest-ranked host and constrains the galaxy luminosity weighting, mildly favoring non-uniform host weights. Fixing the galaxy weighting can instead degrade host identification and even favor the wrong host. This framework recasts dark sirens as a potentially unique probe of the astrophysics of electromagnetically dark compact-binary mergers.

</details>

### [138] [Photoelectron and electron microscopy investigation of laboratory grown Mg-silicate space dust analogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35925)
*Steffen Friis Holleufer,Mie Andersen,Frederik Granzow Doktor,Lars Eric Borchert,Martin Wibrand Larsen,Duncan S. Sutherland,Zheshen Li,Alexei Preobrajenski,Jeppe Vang Lauritsen,Cornelia Jäger,Liv Hornekær,Andrew Cassidy*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用光电效应和电子显微镜，结合X射线光谱与密度泛函理论计算，原位表征了Mg-硅酸盐纳米颗粒薄膜的表面化学结构与形貌演化，发现Mg/Si比和退火处理可调控其组成向镁硅酸盐偏移，但始终存在非化学计量结构，提供潜在催化活性位点；该二维模型更适合作为星际介质中Mg-硅酸盐尘埃的表面科学研究模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星际介质（ISM）中硅酸盐尘埃表面的原子结构在恒星与行星形成区的多种化学过程中起关键作用，需构建更贴近实际的尘埃模型以开展表面催化机制研究。

Method: 采用超高真空（UHV）共沉积法在石墨基底上制备Mg-硅酸盐纳米颗粒薄膜，结合原位光电效应、X射线光电子能谱（XPS）、近边X射线吸收精细结构（NEXAFS）及密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，并辅以异位电子显微镜追踪形貌变化。

Result: Mg/Si比从3.5降至1.8或UHV退火均可促使成分由MgO与Mg-硅酸盐混合态转向以Mg-硅酸盐为主，但始终存在非化学计量结构；电子显微图像显示成分依赖的形貌及其差异化热重构行为；所获二维纳米薄膜可作为激光烧蚀所得ISM类尘埃的更适用表面科学模型。

Conclusion: Mg-硅酸盐纳米颗粒薄膜的组成与结构可通过Mg/Si比和退火调控，其固有的非化学计量性提供了多样化的潜在催化活性位点；该二维模型显著提升了对星际Mg-硅酸盐尘埃表面催化性质的实验可探性。

Abstract: The atomic structure at the surface of interstellar silicate dust particles likely plays a key role in a variety of chemical processes occurring in star- and planet-forming regions of the interstellar medium (ISM). Here, we use the photoelectric effect to characterize, in situ, the local chemical structure of Mg-silicate nanoparticulate films, and ex situ electron microscopy to trace changes to morphology resulting from different starting compositions. Nanoparticulate films are prepared via co-deposition of Si, Mg and O atoms on a graphitic substrate under ultra-high vacuum (UHV) and are characterised with X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy and near edge X-ray absorption fine structure measurements. Analysis, supported by density functional theory calculations, shows that adjusting the Mg-to-Si ratio from 3.5 to 1.8 changes particle composition from a mixture of MgO and Mg-silicate towards predominantly Mg-silicate. Annealing in UHV also pushes the composition towards Mg-silicate, however, non-stoichiometric chemical motifs are always observed, presenting a distribution of potentially catalytically active sites. Electron microscopy images show composition-dependent film morphologies that restructure differently upon thermal annealing. The Mg-silicate nanoparticulate films presented here are characterised as a 2D version of ISM analogue dust particles prepared via laser ablation and are therefore a more readily suitable model for surface science investigations into the catalytic properties of interstellar Mg-silicate dust.

</details>


### [139] [Massive Population III Galaxies Near a GN-z11 Analog: The Role of the Baryon-Dark Matter Streaming Velocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35941)
*Eli Visbal,Greg L. Bryan,Ryan Hazlett*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过半解析模型结合N体模拟，研究了高红移大质量星系（如GN-z11）周围是否可能形成第三星族星（Pop III），发现存在受电离反馈延迟、在Lyman-Werner辐射场中爆发性形成的晚期Pop III星系（如Hebe），其丰度强烈依赖于重子-暗物质流速v_bc；预测在z~11需观测约四个GN-z11类星系才能发现一个此类源，并指出该环境或利于大质量黑洞种子形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST探测到候选Pop III星系Hebe位于z=10.6大质量星系GN-z11附近，引发对高密度环境中Pop III星能否在金属污染下仍形成的根本疑问。

Method: 构建结合Pop III与金属增丰恒星形成的半解析模型，应用于围绕GN-z11类比体的高密度区域zoom-in N体模拟，分辨出可形成第一代恒星的微晕，并考虑电离反馈和Lyman-Werner辐射影响。

Result: 发现多数Pop III形成于z~15前，但存在一类受中央星系电离反馈延迟、位于其附近的晚期Pop III星系，其形成强烈依赖于初态重子-暗物质流速v_bc：v_bc=0时无此类源，高v_bc时显著增多；统计预测z~11每观测~4个GN-z11类星系可发现1个此类源；该环境亦可能孕育大质量黑洞种子。

Conclusion: 高红移大质量星系周围并非完全抑制Pop III形成，特定动力学条件（如高v_bc）下可产生延迟爆发的Pop III星系，Hebe即属此类；该机制为理解早期宇宙恒星与黑洞协同演化提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recently the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) detected Hebe, a Population III (Pop III) candidate located ${\sim}3$ kpc in projection from the massive $z=10.6$ galaxy GN-z11. This raises the question of whether Pop III star formation is expected in the vicinity of massive high-redshift galaxies or whether it would be prevented by early metal enrichment. We address this with a semi-analytic model of Pop III and metal-enriched star formation applied to a zoom-in N-body simulation of a highly overdense region surrounding a GN-z11 analog. The zoom-in region resolves the minihalos where the first stars form. We find that Pop III and metal-enriched star formation both occur earlier, and with a higher star formation rate density, than in a typical mean-density environment. The majority of Pop III star formation in minihalos ends by $z\sim15$; however, we identify a distinct population of late-forming Pop III galaxies that form near the central galaxy. These systems have their star formation delayed due to ionizing feedback from the central GN-z11 analog and experience a Lyman-Werner (LW) intensity of ${\sim}10$ - $100~J_{21}$, potentially leading to massive Pop III starbursts similar to Hebe. Interestingly, the abundance of these Pop III galaxies depends strongly on the baryon-dark matter streaming velocity, $v_{\rm bc}$, because a higher streaming velocity allows halos to remain chemically pristine for longer. None of these late-forming Pop III sources occur for $v_{\rm bc}=0$, while many form at high $v_{\rm bc}$. Averaging over the expected distribution of streaming velocities, we predict that of order four GN-z11-like galaxies would need to be surveyed at $z\sim11$ to expect to find one such source. We also note these environments may provide a natural formation site for heavy black hole seeds.

</details>


### [140] [GA-NIFS view of LAP1: Assembly of Pop~III stellar clusters during EoR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35945)
*J. Scholtz,R. Maiolino,S. Carniani,E. Vanzella,R. J. Terlevich,H. Übler,M. Perna,S. Arribas,E. Bertola,P. Bergamini,T. Böker,A. Bolamperti,A. Bunker,S. Charlot,M. Curti,F. D'Eugenio,C. Grillo,L. R. Ivey,X. Ji,G. C. Jones,M. Meneghetti,M. M. Messa,R. Pascalau,B. Rodríguez Del Pino,P. Rosati,E. Rusta,S. Salvadori,E. Terlevich,G. Venturi,S. Zamora,A. Zanella*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率JWST NIRSpec-IFS观测和对已有JWST与VLT/MUSE数据的再分析，确认LAP1系统具有极低金属丰度（12+log(O/H)=6.5–6.8）和低恒星质量（10³–10⁴ M⊙），其高Hα等效宽度（>1200 Å）和小尺度（<10 pc）支持其为原初星族III（Pop III）恒星形成的早期致密星团候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测红移z~6.6处可能存在的原初星族III（Pop III）恒星，LAP1是其中最具前景的候选体之一。

Method: 利用JWST NIRSpec-IFS高光谱分辨率（R~2700）新观测数据，并结合JWST/NIRCam、JWST/NIRSpec及VLT/MUSE归档数据进行综合再分析；通过谱线强度（如Hβ）、金属丰度标度、Hα等效宽度及动力学质量上限估算等方法开展物理性质诊断。

Result: 确认LAP1金属丰度为12+log(O/H)=6.5–6.8（约1–2%太阳金属量），恒星质量为10³–10⁴ M⊙；A、B子成分的动力学质量上限分别为<10⁶·⁶–¹⁰⁷·⁶ M⊙和<10⁵·⁶–¹⁰⁶·⁶ M⊙；Hα等效宽度>1200 Å，推断恒星年龄为2–5 Myr；系统尺度<10 pc。

Conclusion: LAP1很可能代表在更大但未被探测到的星系中形成的首批致密星团，符合原初星族III恒星形成场景，与其他文献中低金属丰度Pop III候选体一致。

Abstract: Lensed and Pristine 1 (LAP1) is one of the most promising candidates for the detection of so-called Pop~III stars at $z\sim6.6$. In this work, we present new high-spectral-resolution ($R \sim 2700$) \jwst NIRSpec-IFS observations of the system along with a detailed re-analysis of all archival \jwst and VLT/MUSE data. We confirm the very low metallicity of the system, with $12+\log(\text{O/H})=6.5\text{--}6.8$ (1--2\% Z$_{\odot}$), and its low stellar mass, with $M_{*}= 10^{3\text{--}4}$ \Msun, based on the non-detection in \jwst/NIRCam filter stacks and the \Hbeta luminosity. The high-resolution IFS data allow us to constrain the upper limits on the dynamical masses ($\log_{10}(M_{*}/M_{\odot})$) of the A and B components to $<6.6\text{--}7.6$ and $<5.6\text{--}6.6$, respectively. Based on the metallicity and the high equivalent width of \Halpha (EW $>1200$\,Å), we estimate the possible range of the LAP1 stellar population ages to be $2\text{--}5$~Myr, consistent with the first burst of star formation in a pristine environment. Based on the size ($<10$~pc) and mass of the system, we discuss the nature of LAP1 as representing the formation of the first stellar clusters in a larger undetected galaxy, similar to other low-metallicity Pop~III candidates in the literature.

</details>


### [141] [The 1.6 $μ\mathrm{m}$ $\rm{H^{-}}$ bump as a diagnostic of TP-AGB contributions and recent quenching. I. Insights from stellar population models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35969)
*Pengjun Lu,Song Huang,Shiying Lu,Meng Gu,Li Shen,Cheng Li*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了1.6微米H⁻凸起在星系光谱中的应用潜力，尤其关注其作为恒星种群年龄诊断工具及对近期熄火过程研究的价值。该特征受热脉动渐近巨星支（TP-AGB）贡献影响显著，但不同模型预测差异大；作者通过XSL和FSPS模型分析表明，H⁻凸起具有独特的年龄响应，可与光学谱线联合约束熄火历史，但解释依赖于模型假设和恒星形成历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H⁻凸起虽常见于星系光谱，但其在恒星种群定年和研究近期熄火方面的潜力尚未充分挖掘，且不同模型对其预测差异大，亟需厘清驱动机制并评估其诊断能力。

Method: 基于X-shooter光谱库（XSL）和柔性恒星种群合成（FSPS）模型，开展受控实验，分析H⁻凸起对TP-AGB星光贡献和所用模板的依赖性，并结合JWST观测光谱进行对比验证。

Result: H⁻凸起强度强烈依赖TP-AGB贡献及其模板选择；XSL与TP-AGB开启的FSPS模型均预测快速熄火后出现瞬态增强，且其年龄响应不同于光学谱线；含TP-AGB的模型能较好拟合现有JWST观测的H⁻波段。

Conclusion: H⁻凸起是探测近期熄火和检验TP-AGB模型的重要新探针，但其物理解释需谨慎考虑模型依赖性和星族演化历史，值得在当前及未来观测中进一步应用。

Abstract: The 1.6 $μ\mathrm{m}$ $\rm{H^{-}}$ bump arises from a minimum in the opacity of the negative hydrogen ion in cool stellar atmospheres. Although it is recognized as a common feature in galaxy spectra, its potential for studying stellar populations and recent quenching remains little explored. Its enhancement at intermediate ages is strong in some stellar population models with substantial thermally pulsing asymptotic giant branch (TP-AGB) contributions but weak or absent in others. This variation makes the feature both a potential age diagnostic and a source of model uncertainty. We use models based on the X-shooter Spectral Library (XSL) and Flexible Stellar Population Synthesis (FSPS) to examine what drives this behavior and what it can reveal about quenching. Controlled FSPS experiments show that the $\rm{H^{-}}$ bump depends on both the amount of TP-AGB light and the stellar template used to represent it. The adopted XSL and TP-AGB-on FSPS models predict a transient enhancement following rapid quenching, with a different age response from optical spectral features. Combining these diagnostics can therefore provide additional information about recent quenching, although the interpretation depends on the population model and star-formation history. An illustrative comparison with existing JWST spectra shows that models including TP-AGB contributions can describe the observed $\rm{H^{-}}$ region reasonably well. These results motivate further use of the $\rm{H^{-}}$ bump to test TP-AGB prescriptions and investigate quenching with current and forthcoming observations.

</details>


### [142] [COSMOS-3D: Diverse Environments and Hot-dust Signatures among Dusty Star-forming Galaxies at $z$ = 4.9-7.2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35987)
*Siwei Zou,Manuel Aravena,Shaoze Geng,Romain A. Meyer,Jianwei Lyu,Jaclyn B. Champagne,Jia-Sheng Huang,Andreas L. Faisst,Shuqi Fu,Andrew J. Battisti,Hiddo Algera,Caitlin M. Casey,Xiaohui Fan,Maximilien Franco,Ghassem Gozaliasl,Linhua Jiang,Koki Kakiichi,Darshan Kakkad,Zihao Li,Lun-Jun Liu,Felix Martinez,Jorge A. Zavala,Rasha M. Samir*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST和ALMA等多波段数据，研究红移z=4.9–7.2的18个尘埃遮蔽星系（DSFGs），发现其环境密度、形态与热尘辐射存在相位依赖性关联：并合型DSFGs呈现显著致密过密区，而MIRI亮源则缺乏邻近Hα伴星且需额外热尘成分解释其中红外辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尘埃遮蔽星系（DSFGs）被认为是早期大质量晕形成的重要示踪体，但在z>4时，其尘埃遮蔽恒星形成活动、形态结构、热尘辐射与周围环境之间的关联仍不清楚。

Method: 结合JWST/NIRCam F444W光谱、MIRI F1000W/F2100W成像及多个ALMA巡天（CRISTAL、CHAMPS、REBELS、A3COSMOS）选源样本，对18个z=4.9–7.2的DSFGs或候选体进行环境密度分析（采用光谱+测光联合过密度估计器），并与Hα发射线星系样本对比；同时分析形态（并合/相互作用）、中红外辐射特征（如3.3μm PAH与热尘贡献）。

Result: DSFGs在R=0.5 pMpc内平均过密度δ_spec+phot=0.92，高于HAEs（0.80）；并合型DSFGs过密度可达4.70；MIRI亮DSFGs无明显Hα伴星富集，且F2100W流量无法单由3.3μm PAH解释，暗示存在额外热尘成分。

Conclusion: DSFGs的致密发射线核、并合驱动的尘埃遮蔽相与MIRI亮热尘相，在早期结构增长过程中未必空间或时间共存，支持一种相位依赖的演化图景。

Abstract: Dusty star-forming galaxies (DSFGs) are expected to trace early massive-halo assembly, but the connection between dust-obscured star formation, morphology, hot dust, and environment remains unclear at $z$>4. We combine JWST/NIRCam F444W grism spectroscopy from COSMOS-3D with MIRI F1000W/F2100W imaging and a mixed ALMA-selected and ALMA-followed dusty-galaxy sample from CRISTAL, CHAMPS, REBELS, and A3COSMOS to study 18 DSFGs or DSFG candidates at z=4.9-7.2. We compare their environments with the parent spectroscopically confirmed emission-line sample and the COSMOS-Web photo-z galaxy sample using separate and combined overdensity estimators. The parent narrow-line H$α$ sample has a median observed, dust-uncorrected ${\rm SFR}_{\rm Hα}=11.7 M_\odot~{\rm yr^{-1}}$. Within R=0.5 pMpc, DSFGs have a median $δ_{\rm spec+phot}=0.92^{+0.06}_{-0.16}$, compared with $0.80^{+0.05}_{-0.04}$ for HAEs, with a stronger contrast on smaller scales. The strongest compact overdensities are associated with merger/interacting morphology: merging DSFGs reach $δ_{\rm spec+phot}=4.70^{+1.61}_{-1.51}$ within R=0.5 pMpc. In contrast, MIRI-bright DSFGs do not show an enhanced number of nearby visible H$α$-emitting companions, and their F2100W fluxes are difficult to explain with the 3.3 $μ$m PAH feature alone, suggesting an additional hot-dust component. These results are consistent with a possible phase-dependent picture in which the compact line-emitter core, merger-driven dusty phase, and MIRI-bright hot-dust phase need not be spatially or temporally identical during early structure growth.

</details>


### [143] [CHAMPS: The COSMOS High-Redshift ALMA-MIRI Population Survey - A New Census of the Dusty Early Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35992)
*Andreas L. Faisst,Felix Martinez,Jeyhan S. Kartaltepe,Manuel Aravena,Caitlin M. Casey,John D. Silverman,Sune Toft,Ezequiel Treister,Jorge A. Zavala,Hiddo S. B. Algera,Andrew J. Cooper,Simon Hempel-Costello,Ivan Delvecchio,Nicole E. Drakos,Andrea Enia,Steven L.
