Lai,Erini Lambrides,Daizhong Liu,Georgios E. Magdis,Vincenzo Mainieri,Sinclaire M. Manning,Claudia Maraston,Richard Massey,Henry Joy McCracken,Jed McKinney,Wilfried Mercier,Thibaud Moutard,Masafusa Onoue,Louise Paquereau,Alvio Renzini,Jason Rhodes,R. Michael Rich,Brant E. Robertson,Giulia Rodighiero,David B. Sanders,Claudia M. Scarlata,Nick Scoville,Kartik Sheth,Marko Shuntov,Laura Sommovigo,Martin Sparre,Tomoko L. Suzuki,Margherita Talia,Benny Trakhtenbrot,Francesco Valentino,Brittany N. Vanderhoof,Eleni Vardoulaki,Aswin P. Vijayan,Katherine E. Whitaker,Stephen M. Wilkins*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了COSMOS高红移ALMA-MIRI星系巡天（CHAMPS），利用ALMA在z>4宇宙中探测大量尘埃遮蔽星系，包括z>6的候选体，并结合JWST等多波段数据研究早期尘埃形成与演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决星系尘埃在宇宙时间尺度上的形成与演化问题，尤其是挑战当前对再电离时期尘埃贫乏星系的认知，并修正宇宙恒星形成率密度估计。

Method: 开展144小时ALMA 1.2mm（band 6）大样本巡天，覆盖COSMOS场0.2 deg²，结合JWST/NIRCam+MIRI及X射线、紫外、光学、亚毫米和射电等多波段数据，进行盲搜探测与统计堆叠分析。

Result: 探测到约385个高红移尘埃星系、68个尘埃遮蔽AGN及十余个z>6星系；发现多个z≈7.5的高红移、高尘埃质量候选体；实现对JWST识别星系的亚毫米波段堆叠，精确测定尘埃质量和掩蔽恒星形成率；并开展CO和[CII]发射线的盲搜。

Conclusion: CHAMPS为研究早期宇宙尘埃起源、演化及 obscured恒星形成提供了迄今最系统、高灵敏度的观测基础，显著拓展了对z>4尘埃星系种群的认知。

Abstract: Understanding how dust forms and evolves over cosmic time is a key open problem in extragalactic astrophysics. Galaxies in the early Universe were once thought to be mostly dust-poor, but recent discoveries of heavily dust-obscured galaxies near the Epoch of Reionization challenge models of early dust production and raise new questions about the true cosmic star formation rate density. In this paper, we present the COSMOS High-Redshift ALMA-MIRI Population Survey (CHAMPS), which sheds light on the dusty early universe at z>4. CHAMPS is a new 144h ALMA 1.2mm (band 6) large program mapping the combined JWST/NIRCam+MIRI footprint in the COSMOS field. It covers 0.2deg2 with a synthesized beam size of 1.1" and a sensitivity of 0.14mJy/beam. It is complemented, alongside JWST, by ancillary X-ray, UV, optical, sub-mm, and radio data. CHAMPS, optimized as a blind survey, detects above 5$σ$ approximately 385 individual dusty high-redshift sub-mm sources, 68 dusty AGN, and a dozen dusty galaxies at z>6. The galaxies are largely not detected in JWST/F150W, making them prototypical near-IR dark galaxies. We showcase three of the highest-redshift most dusty and massive candidate galaxies detected in CHAMPS with redshifts up to z=7.5. CHAMPS also enables stacks of thousands of galaxies grouped in bins of various physical parameters. Its sub-mm coverage of dust-attenuated JWST-identified galaxies provides robust constraints on dust masses and obscured star formation rates to study early dust formation and evolution. In addition, CHAMPS enables a blind search for luminous CO line emitters at z=0.4-2.7 and [CII]158$μ$m emitters at z=6.7.

</details>


### [144] [A Systematic Assessment of Total Stellar Mass Measurements with Spatially Resolved Photometry and Medium Bands at 1 < z < 9: The Impact of Mass-to-Light Variations and Outshining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35996)
*Naadiyah Jagga,Adam Muzzin,Visal Sok,Vivian Yun Yan Tan,Ghassan Sarrouh,Kartheik Iyer,Yoshi Asada,Chris J. Willott,Maruša Bradač,Nicholas S. Martis,Gaël Noirot,Gregor Rihtaršič,Marcin Sawicki,Sunna Withers,Jacqueline Antwi-Danso,Samantha C Berek,Danilo Marchesini,Maya Merchant,Katherine Myers*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST和HST数据，分析约3000个红移1<z<9星系的恒星质量偏差，发现高比恒星形成率（sSFR）星系中积分光度法系统性低估恒星质量，尤其在低质量星系中；空间分辨光度法可显著减小该偏差，并减弱对中波段滤光片的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究空间分辨与积分光度法在恒星质量估算中的系统性偏差，特别是受星族年龄分布和sSFR影响的程度。

Method: 基于JWST（CANUCS、Technicolor、JUMPS）和HST的UV-IR宽/中波段成像数据，对约3000个星系进行二维参数建模，比较空间分辨与积分恒星质量估计，并分析其与sSFR、质量、质量光比离散度等参数的关系。

Result: 高sSFR（>10^{-8.5} yr^{-1}）星系中积分恒星质量平均偏低Δlog₁₀(M∗)≈−0.36 dex，低质量星系偏差最大；sSFR在10^{-7}–10^{-8} yr^{-1}区间内，质量光比离散度σ[log₁₀(M∗/L_V)]与质量偏差呈显著负相关（ρ_S=−0.37）；仅用宽波段时，空间分辨法的质量偏差（0.15 dex）远小于积分法（0.33 dex）。

Conclusion: 空间分辨光度法能有效缓解因年轻恒星“掩蔽”年老星族导致的恒星质量低估问题，尤其对高sSFR、低质量星系至关重要；同时降低对中波段数据的依赖，提升高红移星系恒星质量测量的可靠性。

Abstract: JWST NIRCam spatially resolved photometry enables studies of galaxies' stellar populations and stellar masses on sub-kpc scales. We use JWST (CANUCS, Technicolor, JUMPS) and HST data, covering wide- and medium-band UV-IR filters to study the stellar mass bias between spatially resolved and integrated photometry of $\sim$3000 galaxies at 1 $<z_{\mathrm{phot}}<$ 9. The 2D parameter maps reveal spatial variations in stellar mass and populations, showing that integrated measurements systematically underestimate stellar masses. We find that the stellar mass bias remains small for galaxies with specific star formation rate $\mathrm{sSFR} < 10^{\mathrm{-9}}$ yr$^{\mathrm{-1}}$, but increases for galaxies with $\mathrm{sSFR} > 10^{\mathrm{-8.5}}$ yr$^{\mathrm{-1}}$. For these high-sSFR galaxies, the median offset reaches $Δ\log_{\mathrm{10}} (M_{\ast})$ $\sim$ -0.36 dex with the largest bias occurring in low-mass ($M_{\ast} \leq 10^{\mathrm{ 9.5}} M_{\odot}$) galaxies. These results are consistent with the outshining of older populations by recent star formation. High-sSFR galaxies also show spatial variations in their mass-to-light ratios ($M_{\ast}/L_{V}$): the stellar mass bias decreases with $σ[\log_{\mathrm{10}}(M_{\ast}/L_{V})]$ for galaxies with $10^{\mathrm{-8}} \leq \mathrm{sSFR}[\mathrm{yr}^{\mathrm{-1}}] < 10^{\mathrm{-9}}$, with the strongest trend for $10^{\mathrm{-7}} \leq \mathrm{sSFR}[\mathrm{yr}^{\mathrm{-1}}] < 10^{\mathrm{-8}}$, quantified by the Spearman correlation coefficient $ρ_{\rm S}=-0.37$. While medium bands are important for constraining stellar masses from integrated photometry, spatially resolved photometry reduces the impact of their absence, with dispersion offsets of $Δ\log_{\mathrm{10}} (M_{\ast})$ = 0.33 dex for integrated photometry but 0.15 dex for resolved photometry when only wide-bands are included.

</details>


### [145] [An Off-Nuclear Tidal Disruption Event Discovered At Late Times: The Case Of TDE 2023mfm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35998)
*Itai Sfaradi,Ryan Chornock,Yuhan Yao,Erica Hammerstein,Jean Somalwar,Raffaella Margutti,Huei Sears,Robert Stein,Natalie LeBaron,Adelle J. Goodwin,Sjoert van Velzen,Matthew J. Graham,Kate D. Alexander,Nayana A. J.,Edo Berger,Jonathan Carney,Yvette Cendes,Collin T. Christy,Walter W. Golay,Wenbin Lu,James C. A. Miller-Jones,Charlotte Ward,Eli Wiston,Tracy X. Chen,Mansi M. Kasliwal,Frank J. Masci,Jesper Sollerman*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报告了光学选源潮汐撕裂事件（TDE）2023mfm的发现与分析，该事件发生在一个偏离宿主星系中心约1.08 kpc的流浪大质量黑洞（~10^6.2 M⊙）上，宿主为一个含低光度AGN的巨椭圆星系；事件表现出典型光学TDE特征，且晚期HST观测暗示TDE位置存在未分辨的恒星团（可能为矮星系残骸），射电辐射延迟一年出现，可能源于延迟外流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究偏离星系中心的流浪大质量黑洞能否产生可探测的潮汐撕裂事件，并理解其宿主环境、辐射特性及多波段行为，以检验TDE形成机制与黑洞动力学模型。

Method: 综合ZTF、Lick、Keck、Swift、Chandra、XMM-Newton、HST和VLA等多台址、多波段观测数据，对TDE 2023mfm进行光变分析、黑体温度拟合、位置偏移测量、光谱建模及射电时序研究。

Result: 确认TDE 2023mfm源自一个质量为10^{6.2±0.5} M⊙、偏离宿主星系中心0.66″（1.08 kpc）的MBH；其光学光变宽（半高全宽达47.8天）、高温持续（T_bb ≈ 22,000 K逾一月）；HST揭示TDE位置存在10^7–10^8 M⊙恒星团；射电辐射延迟至少一年出现。

Conclusion: TDE 2023mfm是首个明确证认来自流浪MBH的光学TDE，证实此类黑洞仍可有效捕获并撕裂恒星；其宿主环境暗示曾经历小并合，为理解星系演化中黑洞迁移与TDE统计提供关键案例；射电延迟现象提示需重新审视TDE喷流/外流启动时标。

Abstract: We present the discovery and analysis of the optically selected tidal disruption event (TDE) 2023mfm, which originates from a wandering massive black hole (MBH) in a massive ($\sim 10^{11} \,$M$_{\odot}$) galaxy hosting a central low-luminosity active galactic nucleus (AGN). Our analysis of the ZTF, Lick, Keck, Swift, Chandra, XMM-Newton, HST, and VLA observations reveals that TDE 2023mfm is a TDE-H from a $10^{6.2 \pm 0.5}\,$M$_{\odot}$ black hole which is offset by $0.66 \pm 0.02$" from the center of its host galaxy, corresponding to a projected distance of $1.08 \pm 0.04$ kpc. TDE 2023mfm displays all the traits of optically selected TDEs. The emission remains hot, $T_{\rm bb} \sim 22,000 \pm 1,000$ K, for more than a month after peak, and the $g$-band light curve peaks at $\left( 2.24^{+0.10} _{-0.11} \right) \times 10^{43} \, \rm erg \, s^{-1}$ and stays above half-maximum luminosity for $47.8^{+3.7}_{-3.5}$ days. The late-time optical-to-UV emission observed with the HST suggests the presence of an unresolved stellar population with a stellar mass of $10^7-10^8\,$M$_{\odot}$ at the position of the TDE, possibly being a low-mass dwarf galaxy or a stripped galaxy from a previous minor merger. The radio emission from TDE 2023mfm emerges at least a year after optical discovery, and we find it likely that it originates from a delayed outflow, however, further observations are needed to determine its true origin.

</details>


### [146] [MOCSS: Multi-Object Coronagraphy for JWST/NIRSpec Slitless Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36003)
*Mason S. Huberty,Claudia Scarlata,Matthew J. Hayes,Alaina Henry,Danielle A. Berg,Vieri Cammelli,Richard Ellis,Anne E. Jaskot,Matilde Mingozzi,Pierluigi Monaco,Alberto Saldana-Lopez,Jonathan C. Tan,Alice Young*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“多目标日冕 slitless 光谱观测”（MOCSS）的新方法，利用JWST/NIRSpec进行高分辨率（R≳1000）、无狭缝光谱观测，通过关闭对应亮源的微快门、其余全开，实现对哈勃超深场的大面积、无预选目标的深度观测；成功获得22个红移1.3<z<4.6的多线发射体，其中18个为首次获得光谱红移，并发现G140M/F100LP一阶光谱延伸至2.94 μm，优于NIRISS WFSS的波长连续性和灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统 slitless 光谱观测中目标预选限制和波长覆盖不连续的问题，提升JWST在1–2 μm波段对遥远星系（尤其弱发射线源）的无偏发现能力。

Method: 利用JWST/NIRSpec G140M/F100LP模式，采用“多目标日冕 slitless 光谱”（MOCSS）策略：全开微快门阵列，仅关闭与已知亮源位置对应的快门，实现高分辨（R≳1000）、大面积、无狭缝光谱观测；结合Hubble Ultra Deep Field数据进行验证与源提取。

Result: 获得22个置信多线发射体（1.3<z<4.6），含[OII]、[OIII]、Hα、Hβ、[NII]等谱线，覆盖约80%直接识别的发射线；首次给出其中18个源的光谱红移；发现一阶光谱红端延伸至2.94 μm；相比同曝光时长的NIRISS WFSS，在1–2 μm波段具备更完整波长覆盖和更高弱线探测灵敏度。

Conclusion: MOCSS是一种高效、高分辨、无预选的 slitless 光谱新范式，显著拓展了JWST在近红外波段对高红移星系的普查能力，为未来大视场、高精度宇宙学巡天提供了关键技术路径。

Abstract: We present an experimental JWST/NIRSpec observational method to obtain $R\gtrsim1000$ G140M/F100LP slitless spectroscopic observations. This "Multi-Object Coronagraphy for Slitless Spectroscopy" (MOCSS) technique involves opening essentially all microshutters on NIRSpec simultaneously while closing shutters corresponding on-sky with known bright objects. Through observations of the Hubble Ultra Deep Field, we demonstrate the discovery potential of these deep, high resolution observations without the need for target pre-selection. We obtain a catalog of 22 secure multi-line emitters at $1.3<z<4.6$, with observations of [OII], [OIII], H$α$, H$β$ and [NII] lines, accounting for $\sim80\%$ of the emission lines identified directly in the dispersed images. The MOCSS survey provides first time spectroscopic redshifts for 18 of these sources. We also demonstrate that first order traces of the NIRSpec/G140M/F100LP observing mode extend substantially beyond the nominal $\sim1.89\rm μm$ red cutoff, as far as $2.94\rm μm$ in the most extreme case. Compared to existing NIRISS WFSS of similar exposure times, we find that the MOCSS technique can observe very faint lines while covering the full $1-2 \rm μm$ regime without gaps in wavelength coverage. This study describes the highest-resolution slitless spectroscopy campaign of the $1-2 \rm μm$ regime and emphasizes the discovery potential of this unorthodox observational technique.

</details>


### [147] [Snapshots of r-process production in the Milky Way disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36013)
*Sarah G. Kane,Stephanie Monty,Madeleine McKenzie,HanYuan Zhang,Sven Buder,Terese Hansen,Tadafumi Matsuno,Jason Sanders,Anke Ardern-Arentsen,Vasily Belokurov,Zofia Kaczmarek,Andrew Garner*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报告了在银河系盘中发现的17颗铕（Eu）增强恒星，其金属丰度较高（[Fe/H] > -0.87），打破了以往Eu增强星主要集中于贫金属晕族恒星的认知；其中14颗为典型的r-II型星（[Ba/Eu] < 0），并发现一颗具有极高[Eu/H] = 1.01的特殊恒星；所有恒星均显示中子俘获元素（如Nd）增强，α元素则与普通盘星一致；部分星体显示双星及s-/i-过程特征；研究还提出适用于盘星的金属度依赖型Eu增强判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高金属丰度环境下r-过程发生机制，突破传统Eu增强星仅存在于贫金属晕族的范式，理解中子俘获核合成在银河系不同成分（尤其是盘）中的作用。

Method: 基于GALAH巡天74,717颗恒星的光谱数据，采用[Eu/Fe] > 0.7筛选Eu增强星，并结合[Ba/Eu]、[Nd/Fe]、α元素等丰度比分析其核合成起源；区分r-II、s-/i-过程候选体；提出金属度依赖的新选择标准。

Result: 发现17颗盘族Eu增强星（-0.87 < [Fe/H] < -0.21），其中14颗为r-II型；首报高金属丰度下r-过程活跃的直接证据；识别出1颗[Eu/H]=1.01的极端个例；确认其化学一致性（α元素正常）与中子俘获元素协同增强（尤其Nd）；发现2颗具双星及Ba/Y强富集特征的候选s-/i-过程星。

Conclusion: r-过程可在银河系盘的高金属丰度环境中发生，挑战了其仅源于早期矮星系并被吸积的传统图景；该样本为研究r-过程产额、场所（如中子星并合）及其在盘演化中的作用提供了关键新探针；提出的金属度依赖选择标准更适用于盘星研究。

Abstract: Stars enhanced in heavy neutron capture elements like europium (Eu) provide a window into the rarest and most exotic nucleosynthetic events. Most Eu-enhanced stars discovered thus far are found among metal-poor populations, with $\mathrm{[Fe/H]}<-1.0$. Here, we report the discovery of $17$ vetted Eu-enhanced stars ($\mathrm{[Eu/Fe]}>0.7$) in the Milky Way disk with metallicities in the range $-0.87<\mathrm{[Fe/H]}<-0.21$ identified from among $74\,717$ stars in the GALAH survey. 14 of these stars additionally have $\mathrm{[Ba/Eu]}<0$, marking enhancement from the rapid neutron capture ($r$-) process and making these r-II stars. Included in their number is a star with a remarkably high $\mathrm{[Eu/H]}=1.01\pm0.09$ at $\mathrm{[Fe/H]}=-0.21$. In addition to Eu, all 17 stars show enhancements in other neutron capture elements, especially in Nd, while otherwise appearing chemically consistent with typical disk stars, particularly in their $α$-element abundances. Two of the Eu-enhanced stars exhibit indications of binarity with strong enrichment in Ba and Y, marking potential signatures of the slow or intermediate neutron capture processes. This sample of 17 Eu-enhanced stars, which span both the high- and low-$α$ disks, are valuable tracers of neutron capture processes, including the $r$-process, at high metallicities. Although we use criteria for Eu-enhanced stars typically applied to the halo to enable comparison to literature studies of $r$-process enhanced stars, we propose a new, metallicity-dependent selection for Eu enhancement that is better-suited to the regime of the Galactic disk.

</details>


### [148] [CHAMPS: Data Reduction and Detection of ~3300 Dusty Galaxies at 1.2 mm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36076)
*Felix Martinez,Jeyhan S. Karaltepe,Andreas L. Faisst,Arianna S. Long,Jorge A. Zavala,Caitlin M. Casey,Jed McKinney,Ezequiel Treister,Maximilien Franco,Sune Toft,Manuel Aravena,John D. Silverman,Hollis B. Akins,Hiddo S. B. Algera,Jacqueline Antwi-Danso,Andrew J. Battisti,Yingjie Cheng,Olivia R. Cooper,Nicole E. Drakos,Carter Flayhart,Fabrizio Gentile,Steven Gillman,Ghassem Gozaliasl,Santosh Harish,Simon Hempel-Costello,Michaela Hirschmann,Olivier Ilbert,Bingcheng Jin,Shuowen Jin,Koki Kakiichi,Thresa Kelly,Zihao Li,Daizhong Liu,Lun-Jun Liu,Sinclaire M. Manning,Henry Joy McCracken,Romain A. Meyer,Louise Paquereau,Maria Pudoka,Jason Rhodes,Brant E. Robertson,Giulia Rodighiero,Rasha M. Samir,David B. Sanders,Marko Shuntov,Margherita Talia,Mattia Vaccari,Francesco Valentino,Aswin P. Vijayan,Feige Wang,Wuji Wang,Can Xu,Jinyi Yang,Yongda Zhu,Siwei Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: CHAMPS是迄今最大的ALMA空白场巡天，覆盖COSMOS场0.2平方度，探测到3355个亚毫米波源，其中376个为高信噪比（≥5σ）可靠样本，中位红移z≈2.54，中位恒星质量log(M⋆/M⊙)≈10.79，并识别出约18%的候选AGN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用COSMOS场丰富的多波段数据，开展大天区、高灵敏度的ALMA 1.2 mm空白场巡天，以系统研究高红移星系和活动星系核（AGN）的性质与演化。

Method: 在COSMOS场开展ALMA 250 GHz（1.2 mm）宽带观测，结合盲搜（SNR≥4.5σ）和MIRI先验引导搜寻（SNR≥3σ）两种方法构建源表；利用光学至射电多波段数据测定光谱/测光红移、恒星质量和AGN标识。

Result: 获得深度达146 μJy beam⁻¹的0.2 deg² mosaic图像，分辨率为1.1″×1.1″；共探测3355个源，定义376个高置信度（≥5σ）科学样本；其平均红移⟨z⟩=2.54，平均恒星质量⟨log(M⋆/M⊙)⟩=10.79，并发现68个（~18%）候选AGN。

Conclusion: CHAMPS提供了迄今最完备的高红移1.2 mm选源样本之一，揭示了z~2.5典型星系的尘埃辐射特性及AGN占比，为理解星系恒星形成与黑洞活动的协同演化提供了关键观测基础。

Abstract: We present the reduction and source catalogs for the \textit{COSMOS High-$z$ ALMA-MIRI Population Survey} (CHAMPS), the largest Atacama Large (Sub)Millimeter Array (ALMA) blank field survey to date at 0.2\,deg$^2$. With observing frequency centered at $250\, $GHz ($1.2\,$mm) and bandwidth of 7.5 GHz, CHAMPS covers the JWST/NIRCam+MIRI footprint in the COSMOS field, taking advantage of the rich, multiwavelength ancillary datasets in COSMOS ranging from X-ray to radio. The majority of the CHAMPS survey (90$\%$) has depths at or below 200 $μ$Jy beam$^{-1}$ and with an average RMS of 146 $μ$Jy beam$^{-1}$. The reduced CHAMPS mosaic has a beam size of $θ_{\rm res} = 1.1'' \times 1.1''$. CHAMPS identifies sources via two detection methods, a blind search for high-SNR sources, and a prior-based search involving MIRI detections to identify faint sources. The blind search yields a total of 595 sources down to an SNR $\ge4.5σ$ while the prior catalog yields a total of 3162 source down to an SNR $\ge3σ$, leading to a full sample of 3355 unique objects detected across both catalogs. We identify a unique population of 385 SNR $\ge5σ$ sources across the two catalogs; after excluding 9 likely spurious edge detections we define a robust science sample of 376 sources above $\ge5σ$. These sources have a median redshift of $\langle z_{1.2 \text{ mm}}\rangle = 2.54^{+0.89}_{-1.69}$ based on optical/near-IR/mid-IR photometric redshifts and high-quality spectroscopic redshifts, and hold a median stellar mass of $\langle\log (\rm M_\star/M_\odot)\rangle = 10.79^{+0.42}_{-3.08}$. We have further identified 68/376 ($\sim18\%$) sources as candidate AGN through X-ray, UV-optical, infrared and radio selection techniques.

</details>


### [149] [MEGATRON: The Physical Origins of Steep UV Slopes at High Redshift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36124)
*Harley Katz,Nicholas Choustikov,Aayush Saxena,Corentin Cadiou,Martin P. Rey,Jeremy Blaizot,Alex J. Cameron,Fergus Cullen,Taysun Kimm,Kosei Matsumoto*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率辐射流体动力学模拟，研究了高红移星系中极蓝紫外光谱斜率（β<-2.8）的物理起源，发现极蓝斜率并不唯一对应高电离光子逃逸率，部分星系因ISM密度低、电离前沿传播慢于大质量恒星主序寿命，导致星云辐射滞后，从而呈现极蓝斜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释高红移星系中观测到的极蓝紫外光谱斜率（β<-2.8）的物理机制，尤其是其是否必然反映高电离光子逃逸率。

Method: 采用具有秒差距分辨率、包含非平衡化学的辐射流体动力学模拟。

Result: 发现两类极蓝星系：一类具有高光子逃逸率；另一类逃逸率<1%，其ISM密度低，电离前沿到达斯特龙根半径的时间长于大质量恒星主序寿命，导致星云辐射滞后。

Conclusion: 极蓝紫外斜率不能唯一指示高电离光子逃逸率，需考虑时间依赖（非平衡）星云辐射效应以正确解读早期宇宙星系光谱。

Abstract: The ultraviolet (UV) spectral slope, $β$, serves as a fundamental probe of stellar population properties and interstellar medium conditions in galaxies. At high redshift, a small sample of galaxies have been confirmed to have extremely blue spectral slopes of $β<-2.8$, as measured directly from spectroscopy. One explanation for such galaxies is that they are leaking copious amounts of ionizing photons such that these photons are never processed into the nebular continuum and we are observing the bluer intrinsic slopes of the stellar populations. Here, we use a detailed set of radiation hydrodynamics simulations with parsec-scale resolution and non-equilibrium chemistry to elucidate the physical origin of extremely blue UV slopes at high redshift. We show that, while some extremely blue galaxies (EBGs) do indeed have high escape fractions, a second population of EBGs has $f_{\rm esc}<1\%$. In this second population, the ISM gas densities tend to be much lower, and the timescale for ionization fronts to reach their Strömgren radii can be longer than the main-sequence lifetime of massive stars, leading to a lag in nebular emission. Our results demonstrate that extremely blue UV slopes do not uniquely imply high escape fractions, which highlights the importance of time-dependent (non-equilibrium) nebular emission for interpreting the spectra of galaxies in the early Universe.

</details>


### [150] [How well do integral-of-motion distribution functions describe Milky Way-analogue halos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36283)
*Jason Kumar,Louis E. Strigari*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了银河系质量暗物质晕的相空间分布，发现f(E,L)比f(E)更能描述模拟结果，但两者均不能高精度完全描述粒子数据，残差可能源于偏离球对称平衡的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解银河系质量暗物质晕的相空间分布，检验其是否能用能量E和角动量L的函数f(E,L)或仅能量的函数f(E)来近似描述。

Method: 基于Auriga数值模拟（含流体动力学与纯暗物质模拟），在球对称平均势中检验粗粒化分布函数对积分运动量E和L的依赖性，并通过残差分析和χ²检验比较f(E)与f(E,L)的拟合优度。

Result: f(E,L)比f(E)更优：中位残差相近，但χ²检验显示角动量依赖具有统计显著性；两类模型均无法高精度拟合，90%分位残差达数十个百分点。

Conclusion: f(E,L)是较有用的近似，但真实暗物质分布存在显著非球对称效应（如各向异性、三轴性、子结构、重子效应），导致现有积分运动量模型无法完全刻画。

Abstract: We study the dark-matter phase space distribution of Milky Way-mass halos in the Auriga simulations, considering both hydrodynamic and dark-matter-only runs. In a spherically averaged potential, we test whether the coarse-grained distribution function can be approximated as a function of the integrals of motion $E$ and $L$. We find that $f(E,L)$ provides a useful description of the simulated halos: although the deviations from this form are formally statistically significant, the median fractional residuals between radial shells are typically $\lesssim 15\%$, with 90th-percentile residuals of order tens of percent. An energy-only approximation, $f(E)$, gives similar median residuals but is systematically disfavored by $χ^2$ tests, indicating that angular momentum dependence is statistically preferred. Ultimately we find that neither $f(E)$ nor $f(E,L)$ fully describes the particle data at high precision. The remaining residuals likely reflect departures from spherical equilibrium, including anisotropy, triaxiality, substructure, and baryonic effects.

</details>


### [151] [Morphological Identification of Dynamical Regimes in MHD Turbulence with ScaleAware-JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36833)
*Mengke Zhao,Guang-Xing Li,Keping Qiu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaleAware-JEPA方法，仅从密度场学习多尺度结构坐标，发现该表示能比单纯密度更清晰地组织湍流星际气体的动力学状态，揭示密度形态本身可作为超越密度幅值的动力学状态坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度幅值无法唯一确定星际湍流气体的动力学状态，需探索能更好反映其湍流、磁场与自引力耦合状态的结构表征。

Method: 采用ScaleAware-JEPA模型，仅以密度场为输入，学习多尺度结构潜变量；将速度场和磁场作为训练后物理诊断工具，分析潜空间中MHD状态的分布及无量纲动力学平衡变化。

Result: 潜空间中类团块、类纤维和类弥散区域展现出有序的湍流速度标度律变化；MHD状态在潜空间中呈有序但重叠分布；关键无量纲动力学平衡量在全潜空间中一致变化。

Conclusion: 仅从密度形态学习到的多尺度表示，其几何结构系统性地受经典MHD诊断量组织，表明多尺度形态是比密度幅值更有效的动力学状态坐标。

Abstract: The morphology of turbulent interstellar gas reflects the coupled action of turbulence, magnetic fields, and self-gravity, but density amplitude alone does not uniquely specify dynamical state. We use ScaleAware-JEPA to learn multiscale structural coordinates from density alone and test whether these learned coordinates organize dynamical states more clearly than density-based conditioning. Clump-like, filament-like, and diffuse-like latent neighborhoods show an ordered progression in turbulent velocity scaling that is not reproduced by density-selected populations. Velocity and magnetic fields are withheld during training and used afterward as post-training physical diagnostics: MHD states occupy ordered but overlapping regions of the learned representation, and dimensionless dynamical balances vary coherently across the full latent atlas. These results show that a representation learned from density morphology can acquire a geometry that is systematically organized by established MHD diagnostics. Multiscale morphology therefore provides a useful coordinate for dynamical state beyond density amplitude alone.

</details>


### [152] [An Ionized Superstructure at Cosmic Dawn Revealed by a Foundation Model for Astrophysical Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36656)
*Minghao Yue,Yongda Zhu,Jiani Ding,Xiaohui Fan,Yufeng Luo*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用为詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜数据训练的基础模型，发现一个红移z=7.567的明亮星系，在z=6.80±0.07处表现出强Lyα透射，延伸达210.5 Mpc（共动），对应极低中性氢丰度（x_HI≈2×10⁻⁶），远低于宇宙平均值（~0.4）；该信号在标准ΛCDM模拟中随机出现概率仅约10⁻⁵，且未见相应星系过密区，与经典“由内而外”再电离模型矛盾，首次体现深度学习发现未知天体物理现象的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙再电离时期的三维空间结构仍缺乏有效约束，亟需新观测手段与分析方法揭示其真实物理图像。

Method: 基于为JWST数据训练的基础模型（foundation model）识别高红移星系光谱特征，并结合Lyα透射测量、中性氢分数估算及ΛCDM宇宙学模拟进行统计检验与物理解释。

Result: 发现z=7.567星系在z=6.80处存在延展达210.5 Mpc的强Lyα透射，对应局部中性氢分数x_HI≈2×10⁻⁶；模拟显示该信号随机发生概率仅≈10⁻⁵；且透射区域无显著星系过密，与标准再电离模型预测不符。

Conclusion: 该观测结果对标准ΛCDM框架下的现有再电离模型构成严峻挑战，暗示可能存在新的再电离物理机制或大尺度电离结构形成过程；同时标志深度学习已成为发现未知天体物理现象的有效新范式。

Abstract: The spatial structure of cosmic reionization remains poorly constrained. Using a foundation model trained for James Webb Space Telescope datasets, we identify a luminous galaxy at redshift $z=7.567$ showing strong Ly$α$ transmission at $z=6.80\pm0.07$. The transmission extends over $210.5^{+55.7}_{-53.5}$ comoving megaparsecs, indicating an ionized superstructure with hydrogen neutral fraction $x_{\rm HI}=(2.07^{+0.29}_{-0.30})\times10^{-6}$, in an epoch when the cosmic mean neutral fraction is $\sim0.4$. Cosmological simulations suggest a probability of $\approx10^{-5}$ for a random sightline to reproduce the signal. We find no significant galaxy overdensity associated with the transmission region, in tension with canonical inside-out reionization models. These observations severely challenge existing reionization models in the standard $Λ$CDM Universe, and provide the first example of deep learning discovering a previously unknown astrophysical phenomenon.

</details>


### [153] [Population search for dark matter spikes in megamaser rotation curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36729)
*Gonzalo Herrera*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出利用水脉泽盘（water megamaser disks）的动力学观测来探测超大质量黑洞周围暗物质晕中可能存在的暗物质尖峰（dark matter spike），通过分析11个已知脉泽盘的旋转曲线，发现仅NGC 4258和NGC 6264在考虑盘面翘曲后仍偏好存在暗物质尖峰，且尖峰质量上限约为黑洞质量的10%；该方法为统计研究黑洞周围暗物质分布提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验绝热增长黑洞在暗物质晕中是否形成理论预言的密度尖峰（坡度1.5–2.5），但目前缺乏直接动力学验证手段。

Method: 利用甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）解析的水脉泽盘旋转曲线反演各半径处的包含质量；构建含暗物质尖峰与不含尖峰的引力势模型进行拟合比较；引入翘曲盘模型以缓解模型简并；辅以模拟注入测试验证灵敏度。

Result: 在11个脉泽盘中，仅NGC 4258和NGC 6264在考虑翘曲后仍显著偏好暗物质尖峰；所有模型下尖峰质量均不超过黑洞质量的约10%；翘曲建模削弱了多数系统的尖峰偏好信号。

Conclusion: 水脉泽盘是探测黑洞邻近暗物质分布的有效探针，具备开展统计性研究的潜力；未来需结合脉泽加速度测量和更内区微弱脉泽探测以打破剩余简并。

Abstract: A black hole growing adiabatically inside a dark matter halo is expected to develop a dense spike, with a power-law slope between 1.5 and 2.5, depending on redshift and baryonic activity. Direct dynamical tests of this prediction are almost non-existent. Here we show that water megamaser disks offer one; their masers orbit within a parsec of the black hole and are mapped individually with very long baseline interferometry, so the enclosed mass at each radius can be read off the rotation curve. We develop a search method and apply it to eleven megamaser disks with public kinematics. Compared with a black hole alone, a spike is preferred in NGC 1194 and NGC 4258 and moderately improves the fit in several others. Allowing the disks to warp removes this preference in all but NGC 4258 and NGC 6264. No combination of disks, warped or not, allows a spike heavier than about ten per cent of the black hole mass, a sensitivity validated with mock injection tests. Mapping the warps with maser accelerations and detecting fainter inner masers, where a spike and a tilt differ most, can break the remaining degeneracy. Megamaser disks open a window onto dark matter around supermassive black holes, and, importantly, one that enables population statistics.

</details>


### [154] [Broadband reverberation mapping of quasars at cosmic noon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36743)
*Juan V. Hernández Santisteban*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用DES巡天数据，对36个红移z~0.7–2的类星体进行宽带反响映射分析，使用PyROA方法同时拟合多波段多年光变曲线，稳健探测到31个源的波段间延迟，发现其紫外谱能分布符合薄盘模型（f_ν ∝ λ^{-1/3}），且Balmer跳变区无显著超出，暗示高爱丁顿比源中宽线区贡献较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紫外波段对区分吸积盘与宽线区对类星体光变的贡献至关重要；观测宇宙正午（z~1–2）的类星体可覆盖更广的黑洞质量和光度范围，有助于理解高爱丁顿比吸积流结构。

Method: 重新分析DES巡天中36条z~0.7–2的多波段多年类星体光变曲线，采用PyROA方法同步拟合四光学波段并插值填补季节性观测间隙，以提高延迟探测精度。

Result: 在31/36个类星体中稳健探测到波段间延迟；整体谱能分布斜率-0.28±0.02，与几何薄盘理论f_ν ∝ λ^{-1/3}一致；Balmer跳变区无明显超出，表明宽线区对反响信号影响较小。

Conclusion: 高爱丁顿比类星体的紫外反响信号主要由吸积盘主导，宽线区贡献有限；LSST未来将观测数千个同类源，需在UV静止参考系中细致建模宽线区贡献以准确推断盘尺度。

Abstract: Broadband reverberation mapping provides a powerful tool to investigate the structure of the accretion flow around supermassive black holes. The ultraviolet region of the quasar spectrum is of particular interest, as it enables the disentanglement of the relative contributions from the accretion disc and the broad-line region to the observed variability of quasars in the local Universe. Observations of quasars at cosmic noon ($z\sim 1-2$) offer the opportunity to probe the UV emission across a diverse population, spanning a broad range of black hole masses and luminosities. Here we re-analysed the 36 multi-year quasar lightcurves from the Dark Energy Survey (DES) between $z\sim0.7-2$ to retrieve interband delays and analyse their spectral energy distribution. Using PyROA, we were able to fit all multiple years of observations and the four optical bands simultaneously, significantly improving the lag detection as this method is able to interpolate over the seasonal gaps. We find robust detections of interband delays in 31/36 quasars, suggesting high-Eddington ratios for a large fraction of objects. Their spectral energy distribution is consistent with a geometrically thin disk $f_ν\proptoλ^{-1/3}$, with an ensemble slope of $-0.28\pm0.02$. The full sample of quasars do not show excess around the Balmer jump region, suggesting the impact of the broad-line region to the reverberation signals in high-Eddington sources could be small as documented in some AGN in the local Universe. The prospect of LSST to observe thousands of quasars in this redshift range should motivate the detailed study of the UV-rest frame of high-Eddington quasars to account for the BLR contribution and properly infer disc sizes.

</details>


### [155] [A Full-Sky SPHEREx Map of Integrated 3.3 and 3.4 μm PAH Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37878)
*Roke Cepeda-Arroita,Clive Dickinson,J. Alberto Rubiño-Martín,Ricardo T. Génova-Santos,Gabriel A. Hoerning,Stuart E. Harper,Debabrata Adak*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用SPHEREx QR2数据首次生成全天域、光谱分辨的3.3 μm芳香族C-H伸缩与3.4–3.5 μm脂肪族复合带辐射通量图，直接拟合谱线而非依赖宽带过剩估算，提升了对星际PAH小颗粒及异常微波辐射载体的追踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全天域PAH示踪器为宽带测量，仅能通过连续谱过剩间接推断谱带发射；而小尺寸PAH（如AME候选载体）需高光谱分辨率直接探测其特征谱带。

Method: 基于SPHEREx QR2 Level-2数据（Detector 4，2.42–3.82 μm，102窄带通道），进行掩模坏像素、扣除黄道光模型与点源（经验PSF）、逆方差加权HEALPix重投影（Nside=4096，2.6′ FWHM），并在每像素上联合拟合连续谱与两个红外谱带。

Result: 发布了首套全天域、光谱分辨的PAH 3.3 μm与3.4–3.5 μm带辐射通量图（含总带辐射主产品及其芳香/脂肪族成分分解估计），附可靠性掩模与系统误差说明，并已公开发布。

Conclusion: 该数据集可直接示踪最小PAH颗粒群，比宽带代理更精准刻画其空间分布与物理状态，为研究光解离区、尘埃演化和恒星形成提供新工具，并有助于厘清异常微波辐射起源。

Abstract: Polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbons (PAHs) are ubiquitous in the interstellar medium and are among its smallest carbonaceous grains. Their infrared emission bands trace this small-grain population together with the interstellar radiation field that excites it, and are widely used to diagnose photodissociation regions, dust processing and star formation. We present full-sky maps of the integrated radiance of the 3.3 $μ$m aromatic C-H stretch and the adjacent 3.4-3.5 $μ$m aliphatic complex, from SPHEREx QR2 Level-2 data. Existing all-sky PAH tracers are broadband, so the band emission can only be inferred as an excess over stellar and continuum emission; SPHEREx samples the spectrum finely enough for the bands to be fitted directly. We mask flagged detector pixels, subtract the per-pixel zodiacal-light model, subtract point sources with an empirically derived PSF, and coadd the results into inverse-variance-weighted HEALPix maps at 2.6' FWHM and Nside=4096 in 102 narrowband channels across Detector 4 (2.42-3.82 $μ$m). At every sky pixel we fit a continuum and the two bands. The combined band radiance is the primary product; its decomposition into the aromatic and aliphatic components is released alongside it as an estimate, with its modelling systematic stated. These are the first full-sky maps of PAH band emission, resolving the 3.3 and 3.4-3.5 $μ$m bands spectrally, and the first full-sky PAH maps from SPHEREx to be released. Because the 3.3 $μ$m band is emitted preferentially by the smallest PAHs, the maps trace directly a grain population, including the candidate carriers of anomalous microwave emission, that broadband PAH proxies reach only indirectly. The maps and their associated products, including a reliability mask, are publicly available with an explanatory supplement at https://research.iac.es/proyecto/radioforegroundsplus/pages/data-products/spherex-pahs.php.

</details>


### [156] [Testing KMTNet--PRIME Optical--Near-Infrared Source-Color Constraints in KMT-2024-BLG-0211 and KMT-2024-BLG-1522](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36768)
*Kansuke Nunota,Jennifer C. Yee,Takahiro Sumi,Ryusei Hamada,Ian A. Bond,Andrew Gould,Weicheng Zang,David P. Bennett,Aparna Bhattacharya,Kotaro Daimon,Yuki Hirao,Stela Ishitani Silva,Shuma Makida,Shota Miyazaki,Tutumi Nagai,Seiya Nakayama,Ryo Ogawa,Ryunosuke Oishi,Hideaki Ose,Nicholas J. Rattenbury,Yuki K. Satoh,Daisuke Suzuki,Takuto Tamaoki,Motohide Tamura,Sean K. Terry,Chihiro Ueda,Aikaterini Vandorou,Hibiki Yama,Michael D. Albrow,Sun-Ju Chung,Cheongho Han,Kyu-Ha Hwang,Youn Kil Jung,Yoon-Hyun Ryu,In-Gu Shin,Yossi Shvartzvald,Hongjing Yang,Dong-Jin Kim,Chung-Uk Lee,Byeong-Gon Park*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文分析了2024年两个微引力透镜事件KMT-2024-BLG-0211和KMT-2024-BLG-1522，结合KMTNet光学与PRIME近红外观测数据，重点探讨了多波段颜色（如I−H、V−I等）对源星角半径、透镜物理参数及双透镜解的约束能力与系统偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升微引力透镜事件中源星性质和透镜物理参数（质量、距离）的测量精度，检验多波段颜色联合分析对有限源效应和视差效应的约束能力，并评估经验颜色-颜色关系在实际观测中的适用性。

Method: 基于KMTNet（光学）与PRIME（近红外）联合观测数据，对两个微透镜事件进行光变建模与颜色分析；利用I−H、V−I、V−H、J−H等多种颜色组合反推源星角半径；比较不同颜色约束下所得透镜模型参数的一致性，并将推断的源星颜色与经验颜色-颜色关系对比。

Result: KMT-2024-BLG-0211支持低质量恒星透镜模型，但视差约束弱导致质量与距离不确定；KMT-2024-BLG-1522获得稳健的等质量双透镜解，但源星物理参数依赖所选颜色——不同颜色推得的角半径不同；其(V−I)与(I−H)颜色偏离经验关系，但仍处于恒星观测弥散范围内。

Conclusion: 多波段颜色联合分析可增强微透镜参数约束，但源星颜色不确定性仍是关键系统误差来源；需更大样本统计验证此类偏离的普遍性，并建议通过自适应光学后续观测直接测量透镜-源相对自行与透镜光度以进一步确认。

Abstract: We present analyses of two 2024 microlensing events, KMT-2024-BLG-0211 and KMT-2024-BLG-1522, jointly observed by KMTNet in the optical and PRIME in the near-infrared. For KMT-2024-BLG-0211, the KMTNet--PRIME $I-H$ color provides a useful constraint on the angular source radius for a short-timescale finite-source event with a giant source. The event is consistent with a low-mass stellar lens, although the weak parallax constraint leaves the lens mass and distance uncertain. For KMT-2024-BLG-1522, we compare the results obtained from $V-I$, $I-H$, $V-H$, and $J-H$ color constraints. The microlensing parameters are nearly identical among the four analyses, yielding a robust binary-lens solution with nearly equal masses. However, the inferred source properties and lens physical parameters depend on the adopted source color because the different color estimates imply different angular source radii. Comparing the $(V-I)_{\rm KMT}$ versus $(I-H)_{\rm KMT,PRIME}$ plane shows that the inferred source colors lie off empirical color--color relations but within the scatter of observed stars. The prevalence of such deviations should be tested with a larger sample of KMTNet--PRIME events and this specific case can be further tested with future adaptive-optics follow-up, which can directly measure the lens--source relative proper motion and lens flux.

</details>


### [157] [Detecting HI Self-Absorption using Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38042)
*Eric G. M. Muller,Naomi M. McClure-Griffiths,Hiep Nguyen,Matthew J. Alger,Frances Buckland-Willis,J. R. Dawson,Min-Young Lee,Antoine Marchal*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级卷积神经网络，用于自动、高效地检测中性氢（HI）21-cm谱线中的自吸收特征，无需依赖背景连续谱或对发射轮廓建模，在合成和实测数据上均表现优异，并具备实时处理能力，适用于SKA等新一代射电望远镜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HI自吸收是探测冷原子氢最直接的观测示踪手段，但现有识别方法依赖主观判断或复杂建模，难以实现可重复、系统化和大样本应用。

Method: 设计了一个轻量级卷积神经网络，仅基于合成HI 21-cm谱线数据训练，用于检测自吸收特征并结合后处理推断其速度，不假设底层发射形态，也不使用辅助数据。

Result: 模型在独立合成测试集上达到96.5%准确率、96.0%精确率和97.0%召回率；应用于银河系银道面Riegel-Crutcher云和巨分子纤维的实际观测数据，成功复现已知自吸收结构的空间分布与速度，并与13CO数据一致；单消费级GPU可达约15,000条谱线/秒的实时处理速度。

Conclusion: 该方法为冷HI气体的大规模、自动化、实时探测提供了可靠新工具，显著提升了未来射电巡天（如SKA）的数据分析能力。

Abstract: Cold atomic hydrogen plays a crucial role in the life cycle of interstellar gas. It serves as the intermediary phase in the condensation and cooling processes that bridge the warm diffuse gas in and around galaxies, and the cold molecular gas that drives star formation. HI self-absorption in the 21-cm emission line is the most direct observational tracer of cold HI gas that does not require a continuum background source, yet its systematic extraction remains a long-standing challenge. Existing methods of self-absorption identification rely on subjective by-eye inspection or modelling of the underlying emission, making repeatable, thorough, and large-scale applications difficult. We present a lightweight convolutional neural network designed to detect self-absorption features and infer their velocities via a post-hoc process, without assumptions about the underlying emission or the use of ancillary data. Trained on synthetic emission spectra, the neural network achieves 96.5 per cent accuracy, 96.0 per cent precision, and 97.0 per cent recall on held-out synthetic data. Applied to observed 21-cm emission data from the Riegel--Crutcher cloud and giant molecular filament regions towards the Galactic Plane, the network recovers the spatial distributions and velocities of known self-absorption structures when compared to previous analyses and ancillary 13CO emission data. Crucially, the neural network is computationally efficient, processing detections for ~15,000 spectra per second on a single consumer laptop GPU, enabling real-time cold HI detection at the data rates anticipated by next-generation facilities such as the Square Kilometre Array.

</details>


### [158] [Dynamical friction vs. subhalo heating in Cold Dark Matter haloes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36796)
*Jorge Peñarrubia,Pierfrancesco Di Cintio,Eduardo Vitral,Matthew G. Walker*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗物质晕中大质量恒星系统的轨道演化，指出动力学摩擦与暗物质子晕引起的随机加热之间的平衡决定了星团是向内沉降还是向外扩散，并提出了一个临界质量M_crit；在矮椭球星系（dSph）质量范围的暗物质晕中，M_crit ≳ 10⁵ M⊙，可解释Fornax星系中球状星团未快速沉降的‘计时问题’。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Fornax矮椭球星系中球状星团未按经典动力学摩擦理论预期快速沉降至中心的‘Fornax计时问题’，探究暗物质子结构引起的随机加热是否能显著延缓其轨道衰减。

Method: 结合解析推导与数值实验，分析暗物质晕中恒星系统所受的动力学摩擦（耗能）与子晕引力涨落引起的随机加热（供能）之间的竞争关系，推导并量化临界质量M_crit及其依赖因素。

Result: 发现存在一个临界质量M_crit，使得M_cl > M_crit时星团向内沉降，M_cl < M_crit时向外扩散；M_crit主要由子晕质量函数上限决定，在矮椭球星系质量范围的晕中M_crit ≳ 10⁵ M⊙，与Fornax中球状星团质量相当。

Conclusion: 暗物质子结构引发的随机加热可显著延迟球状星团的轨道衰减，为Fornax计时问题提供一种自然的物理解释；该平衡效应在不同暗物质晕间具有显著散射，源于大质量子晕的稀有性。

Abstract: The orbital evolution of massive stellar systems embedded in dark matter haloes is governed by competing processes. Dynamical friction removes orbital energy, whereas fluctuations in the gravitational field generated by dark matter subhaloes inject energy through stochastic heating. We investigate the balance between these two mechanisms using analytical arguments and numerical experiments. We show that, for a given host halo and subhalo population, there exists a critical mass $M_{\rm crit}$ at which heating and friction balance. Clusters with masses $M_{\rm cl}>M_{\rm crit}$ lose orbital energy and sink towards the centre of the halo, whereas those with $M_{\rm cl}<M_{\rm crit}$ gain energy and expand outwards. At $M_{\rm cl}\simeq M_{\rm crit}$, the two processes balance, leading to a cluster population that, on average, neither sinks nor expands within the host halo. In CDM haloes, the critical mass is primarily controlled by the upper end of the subhalo mass function. Since massive subhaloes are intrinsically rare, the balance between heating and friction shows substantial halo-to-halo scatter. For dark matter haloes in the mass range associated with dwarf spheroidal galaxies (dSphs), we find $M_{\rm crit}\gtrsim 10^5M_\odot$, comparable to the masses of globular clusters in the Fornax dSph. Stochastic heating by dark substructure may therefore significantly delay the orbital decay of globular clusters and help alleviate the Fornax timing problem.

</details>


### [159] [MEGATRON: Dwarf Galaxy Quenching in the Epoch of Reionization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37188)
*David Attard,Ilian T. Iliev,Martin P. Rey,Harley Katz,Corentin Cadiou,Nicholas Choustikov*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过MEGATRON高红移模拟套件，对比四种恒星形成与反馈模型，研究再电离时期低质量星系（M* ~ 10⁴–10⁸ M⊙, z=8.5）的恒星质量增长调控机制，发现反馈强度和聚集性显著影响星系的'活动-静止'占空比与静止比例，并揭示两种物理上不同的熄火机制；同时指出JWST观测存在对活动相的强选择效应，建议利用引力透镜场测量静止星系分数以检验反馈模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低质量星系在再电离时期可能呈现爆发式恒星形成与长期静止交替的循环，但该循环的调控机制及其对恒星反馈建模的敏感性尚不明确。

Method: 基于MEGATRON高红移zoom模拟套件，对相同初始条件采用四种不同的恒星形成与恒星反馈模型进行演化，分析z=8.5时质量为10⁴–10⁸ M⊙星系的恒星质量增长行为，并按最近10 Myr或100 Myr是否显著形成恒星将星系分为活动、迷你熄火和熄火三类。

Result: 反馈越强或越集中，星系熄火比例越高；不同模型在固定质量下的占空比差异达数倍；熄火星系冷气体分布呈现两类物理机制：机械性气体驱散（致密、贫冷气）与热力学抑制（保留冷气但无法成星）；当前JWST/NIRCam非透镜观测难以探测该质量范围的熄火星系，导致通量限制样本严重偏向活动相。

Conclusion: 恒星反馈模型显著决定低质量高红移星系的活动-静止循环特征；利用引力透镜场测量不同恒星质量下熄火星系的比例，可为宇宙黎明时期的反馈物理提供直接观测检验。

Abstract: Low-mass galaxies during the Epoch of Reionization are expected to form stars in short bursts separated by periods of inactivity, but what regulates this cycle, and how sensitive it is to the modelling of stellar feedback, remains unclear. We use the MEGATRON high-redshift zoom simulation suite, in which four star-formation and stellar-feedback prescriptions are evolved from identical initial conditions, to study the regulation of stellar mass growth in galaxies with $M_{*} \sim 10^{4} - 10^{8} M_{\odot}$ at $z = 8.5$. We classify galaxies as Star-Forming, Mini-Quenched (no significant star formation over the last 10 Myr), or Quenched (no significant star formation over the last 100 Myr). The feedback prescription controls both how often galaxies are quiescent and how this depends on mass. Models with stronger or more clustered feedback yield larger quiescent fractions compared to the fiducial and Varying IMF models. Duty cycles at fixed mass also differ by factors of several between models. The cold gas profiles of Quenched galaxies are consistent with two physically distinct modes of quenching: mechanical gas evacuation, producing compact, cold gas-poor systems, and thermodynamic suppression, retaining a cold but star-formation-inactive reservoir. At these masses, quiescent galaxies fall below un-lensed JWST/NIRCam detection limits, whereas star-forming galaxies of the same mass do not, and consequently flux-limited samples will be biased towards active phases. Measuring the quiescent fraction as a function of stellar mass in lensing fields would provide a direct test of stellar-feedback models at Cosmic Dawn.

</details>


### [160] [The ALPINE-CRISTAL-JWST Survey: Investigating the role of interstellar dust, gas and stars in high-z galaxies at kpc-scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37358)
*F. Lopez,M. Relano,I. De Looze,A. L. Faisst,R. Herrera-Camus,R. A. Jorgenson,E. Pérez-Montero,M. Palla,C. Accard,R. O. Amorin,M. Aravena,R. J. Assef,A. J. Battisti,M. Boquien,E. da Cunha,P. Dam,R. L. Davies,M. Dessauges-Zavadsky,A. Ferrara,S. Fujimoto,M. Ginolfi,N. Gutierrez-Vera,A. Hadi,E. Ibar,H. Inami,A. M. Koekemoer,L. L. Lee,J. Li,J. Molina,A. Nanni,D. Narayanan,F. Pozzi,F. Rizzo,M. Romano,D. B. Sanders,P. Sawant,L. Vallini,S. A. van der Giessen,V. Villanueva,G. Zamorani*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用ALMA和JWST/NIRSpec数据，对z~4-5星系中气体、尘埃与恒星的分布及演化进行空间分辨分析，发现尘埃与气体质量比远高于本地星系，且外流显著影响尘埃与气体含量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全面理解星系演化需深入分析其中气体与尘埃的分布与演化，尤其在高红移（z~4–5）时期。

Method: 结合ALMA观测的[CII]线与尘埃连续谱（空间分辨~kpc），以及JWST/NIRSpec IFU数据，推导原子/分子气体质量、尘埃质量、恒星质量、SFR和金属丰度的空间分布。

Result: 氧丰度变化达1 dex；常用原子气体标定方法高估表面密度1–2个量级，而基于[CII]的空间分辨转换因子更可靠；有外流迹象的星系具有更低的尘埃/恒星面密度比与气体质量分数；部分星系尘埃/气体与尘埃/恒星面密度比比本地星系高近一个数量级。

Conclusion: 高红移星系已在约1 Gyr内快速形成大量尘埃；外流不仅驱出气体，也带出尘埃，支持恒星反馈或辐射压导致尘埃清除的理论，可解释其高UV光度。

Abstract: A comprehensive understanding of galaxy evolution requires a detailed analysis of gas and dust, their distribution, and evolution within galaxies. We analyse spatially resolved (~kpc) rest-frame [CII] and dust continuum ALMA observations together with JWST/NIRSpec IFU data of a sample of galaxies at z~4-5. We focus on deriving the spatial distributions of atomic and molecular gas masses, dust and stellar masses, star formation rates (SFR), and metallicity. The oxygen abundance maps have values in the range of 12+log(O/H)~8.1-8.3 with variations of the order of 1dex within the same galaxy. The calibration used in this study to estimate the atomic gas mass in our galaxies seems to overestimate the atomic gas mass surface densities up to 1-2 orders of magnitude. However, a calibration based on a spatially resolved [CII] conversion factor gives a more robust estimation of the total gas mass. Galaxies exhibiting signatures of strong outflows have lower dust-to-stellar surface mass density ratio and lower gas mass fractions than the galaxies with no outflow signatures. While the latter is consistent with these galaxies ejecting part of their gas reservoir to the circumgalactic medium, the low dust-to-stellar surface mass density ratios also suggest that part of the dust content is expelled outside the galaxy, which reinforces previous theoretical suggestions of dust removal by stellar feedback or radiation pressure to explain the high UV luminosities in galaxies at very high-redshifts. Some galaxies of our sample have almost an order of magnitude higher dust-to-gas and dust-to-stellar surface mass density ratio than galaxies in the local Universe, suggesting that a large dust reservoir has been built up already at time scales of ~1 Gyr in these galaxies.

</details>


### [161] [Caught in the act: diffuse stellar and Ly$α$ emission around a halo-less quasar in the assembling cluster CARLA J0800+4029 at z=2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37445)
*Sofia G. Gallego,Simona Mei,Emanuele Daddi,Gaël Noirot,Dominika Wylezalek,R. Michael Rich*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用KCWI光谱和HST成像研究z~2的星系团CARLA J0800+4029，发现强Lyα发射不围绕射电噪类星体，而是偏移并同潮汐结构、弥散星内光（IHL）高度重合，表明气体与恒星在并合过程中协同再分布；类星体位置缺乏Lyα晕，暗示其近邻气体被电离、耗尽或抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高红移星系团中冷气体与星系共同组装过程，特别是Lyα辐射如何示踪气体分布及其与AGN、星系结构的关系。

Method: 结合KCWI宽视场积分场光谱与HST/F140W成像，对z=1.986的CARLA J0800+4029星系团进行深度观测，测量Lyα空间分布、表面亮度、运动学及与IHL、潮汐特征的关联。

Result: 未在类星体位置探测到延展Lyα发射（3σ上限L_Lyα<4.20×10^42 erg/s），而最强Lyα发射（L_Lyα=1.42×10^43 erg/s）显著偏移，并与潮汐结构、IHL及吸收特征一致；Lyα线宽FWHM~300–500 km/s，IHL占比18.8%。

Conclusion: Lyα主导区与AGN解耦，紧密关联于动力学扰动区域，揭示星系团组装中气体与恒星协同再分布；AGN附近缺乏Lyα晕支持其环境气体被强烈反馈影响。

Abstract: Extended Ly$α$ emission is commonly detected around luminous quasars at $z\sim2$-3 and traces cold gas in the circumgalactic environment of the AGN. Together with the spatial distribution of galaxies, Ly$α$ probes gas and galaxy assembly in high-redshift overdensities. We use deep KCWI wide-field spectroscopy and HST/F140W imaging to investigate the assembling cluster CARLA J0800+4029 at $z=1.986\pm0.014$. No robust extended Ly$α$ emission is detected at the radio-loud quasar position. Instead, the dominant Ly$α$ emission is offset from the AGN and coincides with a disturbed structure composed of galaxies, tidal features, and diffuse intra-halo light (IHL). The detected structure has $L_{\rm Lyα}=1.42\times10^{43}$ erg/s and reaches surface-brightness levels of $\sim10^{-17}$ erg/s/cm$^2$/arcsec$^2$. At the quasar position, a matched $3σ$ limit gives $L_{\rm Lyα}<4.20\times10^{42}$ erg/s, a factor of $\sim3.4$ below the detected offset emission and among the most stringent constraints at similar redshift. The Ly$α$ emission is dominated by one extended region near systemic velocity, with FWHM $\sim300$-500 km/s. The associated tidal structure has an IHL fraction of 18.8%, and contains a spatially coherent absorption-like decrement at $\sim+1000$ km/s. The offset and alignment between Ly$α$ emission and IHL suggest that gas and stars have been redistributed together during assembly. The lack of a comparable Ly$α$ halo around the quasar further suggests that gas in the immediate AGN environment is ionized, depleted, or suppressed, while the offset region remains favorable for Ly$α$ emission. CARLA J0800+4029 reveals baryon redistribution during cluster assembly, with the dominant observable Ly$α$ reservoir decoupled from the central AGN and linked to ongoing tidal assembly.

</details>


### [162] [Fooled by mass? Recovering the $Γ-λ_{Edd}$ relation of quasars from their X-ray variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37607)
*Matilde Signorini,Andrea Sacchi,Bartolomeo Trefoloni,Matteo Guainazzi,Elisabeta Lusso,Emanuele Nardini,Chiara Niccolai,Federica Ricci,Anastasia Shlentsova,Mateusz Rałowski*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过分析SDSS DR16Q与4XMM-DR14交叉样本中7835个I型类星体，发现X射线光子指数Γ与爱丁顿比λ_Edd之间存在微弱但真实的相关性（a_λ≈0.20–0.25），该关系仅能通过多历元观测、利用单个源的光变消除黑洞质量系统误差后可靠揭示；传统单次观测回归因质量测量误差导致稀释效应而失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决以往研究中因黑洞质量测量误差大而导致Γ–λ_Edd关系被掩盖或误判的问题，厘清类星体X射线谱形与吸积率的真实物理关联。

Method: 1）构建SDSS DR16Q与4XMM-DR14交叉大样本（7835个源），排除观测偏差；2）对比不同宽线区质量指标（Hβ/MgII/CIV）和bolometric校正下的Γ–logλ_Edd回归；3）创新性采用XMM-Newton多历元观测数据（N=893），对每个源拟合Γ–logL_{2keV}关系，再聚合后验分布以规避黑洞质量不确定性。

Result: 单次观测回归斜率普遍平坦（受回归稀释影响）；多历元方法得到群体平均响应a_X = 0.35±0.03（高质量Γ时达≈0.43），对应a_λ ≈ 0.20–0.25；证实Γ–λ_Edd关系存在但极弱。

Conclusion: Γ–λ_Edd关系真实存在但较弱，当前λ_Edd测量精度不足以单独解释类星体Γ的多样性；必须借助个体源光变消除黑洞质量系统误差，才能可靠揭示该关系。

Abstract: We measure the relation between the X-ray photon index $Γ$ and the Eddington ratio $λ_{Edd}$ in unobscured type-1 quasars, using as parent sample the cross-match of the SDSS DR16Q catalogue with the 4XMM-DR14 serendipitous catalogue. After cleaning the sample of potential observational biases, we obtain 7835 objects, one order of magnitude larger than most previous analyses in the literature. A single regression of $Γ$ against $\logλ_{Edd}$, with single-epoch virial black-hole masses, returns slopes that depend on the mass indicator (H$β$, MgII, CIV) and on the bolometric correction, and that are generally flat. We interpret this as regression dilution: the systematic uncertainty on virial masses is much larger than the intrinsic spread of $λ_{Edd}$ in SDSS quasars. We therefore introduce a different approach, which removes the black-hole mass from the problem. For quasars observed by XMM-Newton at multiple epochs, the mass of each object is fixed, and changes in its X-ray luminosity trace changes in its accretion state. We fit the $Γ$-$\log(L_{2keV})$ relation of each object (for a sample of N=893) and combine the per-object posteriors. This recovers the mean response of the population $a_X = 0.35\pm0.03$, which steepens to $\approx0.43$ when only epochs with precisely measured photon indices are used. Translated to the Eddington ratio, this corresponds to $a_λ\approx0.20$-0.25, depending on the choice of bolometric correction and on the LX-LUV slope. The $Γ$--$λ_{Edd}$ relation therefore exists, but it can be uncovered only by exploiting the variability of individual sources, which removes the black-hole mass from the problem. Moreover, its shallowness implies that the differences in Eddington ratio, as currently measured, cannot by themselves account for the variety of photon indices observed among unobscured quasars.

</details>


### [163] [GA-NIFS: sRMS, a new method for disentangling resolved and unresolved emission in integral field spectroscopy. Application to distant quasars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37625)
*Michele Perna,Santiago Arribas,Carlota Prieto-Jiménez,Isabella Lamperti,Lorenzo Ulivi,Hannah Übler,Giovanni Cresci,Bruno Rodríguez Del Pino,Torsten Böker,Andrew J. Bunker,Stéphane Charlot,Roberto Maiolino,Chris J. Willott,Ivan Delvecchio,Elena Bertola,Madeline A. Marshall,Eleonora Parlanti,Pablo G. Pérez-González,Giacomo Venturi,Sandra Zamora,Filippo Mannucci,Quirino D'Amato,Alessandra De Rosa,Ismael García-Bernete,Miguel Pereira-Santaella,Fabio Rigamonti,Martina Scialpi,Paola Severgnini,Cristian Vignali,Maria Vittoria Zanchettin*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的空间均方根（sRMS）技术，利用部分重叠孔径提取的光谱空间方差，从高红移宽线型活动星系核（BL-AGN）的积分场光谱（IFS）中分离出非核区域发射，并改善其积分谱分解的准确性。该方法在JWST/NIRSpec观测的两个z~6.5类星体上验证有效，显著降低了多成分谱拟合的简并性，并具备探测亚千秒差距尺度双AGN的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型1活动星系核（AGN）的发射线轮廓解释常受明亮、空间不可分辨的核连续谱和宽线区（BLR）发射主导，掩盖了来自窄线区（NLR）和寄主星系的较窄、混合成分；即使使用积分场光谱（IFS），因相关空间变化尺度小于角分辨率（尤其对遥远紧凑寄主星系），仍难以分辨。

Method: 提出空间均方根（sRMS）技术：基于围绕不可分辨核的多个部分重叠孔径提取光谱，计算其空间方差以抑制空间不变发射、保留空间可变成分；将该方法应用于JWST/NIRSpec对两个z~6.5类星体的观测数据；对sRMS谱用多高斯模型拟合以获取空间可变成分的运动学参数，并将这些参数作为约束用于积分谱建模。

Result: sRMS技术可在约5倍优于NIRSpec点扩散函数（PSF）的尺度上识别空间可变的窄线发射，稳健约束各运动学成分的速度场；施加这些运动学约束后，积分谱多组分分解的简并性显著降低；模拟表明该方法可探测投影距离低至~200 pc、BLR流量比约10的z~6.5密近双BL-AGN。

Conclusion: sRMS是一种适用于单历元IFS数据的有效新分析方法，能提升高红移BL-AGN非核发射的空间与运动学解析能力，缓解积分谱分解的简并问题，并有望用于探测亚千秒差距尺度的双AGN系统。

Abstract: The interpretation of AGN emission-line profiles in type1 AGN is often complicated by the dominance of bright, spatially unresolved nuclear continuum and broad-line region (BLR) emission over narrower and blended components from the narrow-line region (NLR) and the host galaxy. This remains challenging even with integral-field spectroscopy (IFS), as the relevant spatial variations can occur on scales smaller than the angular resolution, particularly in the compact host of distant AGN. We introduce the spatial root-mean-square (sRMS) technique, a new approach to analyse single-epoch IFS that uses the spatial variance of spectra extracted from partially overlapping apertures to isolate off-nuclear emission of high-z BL-AGN, and investigate whether the resulting kinematic information can improve the decomposition of their integrated spectra. We construct sRMS spectra from ensembles of overlapping apertures centred on the unresolved nucleus. Emission that is spatially invariant on the scales probed by the apertures is consequently suppressed, whereas spatially varying emission is retained. We apply the procedure to JWST/NIRSpec data of two z~6.5 QSOs. We model the resulting sRMS spectra with multiple Gaussian components to determine the kinematics of the spatially varying emission, and use these kinematic constraints to model the integrated spectra. The sRMS technique identifies spatially varying narrow-line emission on scales up to ~5x smaller than the NIRSpec PSF and provides robust constraints on the kinematics of individual kinematic components. Imposing these kinematic constraints on the integrated-spectrum fit reduces significantly the degeneracy of multi-component decompositions. Simulations further demonstrate the potential of the method to detect spatially offset BLR emission from close dual BL-AGN, down to projected separations of ~200 pc at z~6.5 for a BLR flux ratio of ~10.

</details>


### [164] [Thermally irreversible sulfur chemisorption on silicate grains as a major sulfur reservoir in molecular clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37663)
*Ni-En Sie,Masashi Tsuge,Yasuhiro Oba,Germán Molpeceres,Kenji Furuya,Naoki Watanabe*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验发现硫原子在硅酸盐尘埃表面发生强化学吸附，即使在无定形固态水升华后仍保持结合，导致其在观测中不可见；模型表明至少10%的分子云中硫总量被锁定在硅酸盐尘埃上，为‘缺失硫’问题提供了物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决星际介质中长期存在的‘缺失硫’问题，即分子云中大量宇宙硫元素尚未被识别，需厘清硫在星际尘埃表面的行为。

Method: 采用原位激光探测技术，在模拟星际分子云条件下，实验研究硫原子在天体物理相关硅酸盐基底和无定形固态水（ASW）表面的吸附过程。

Result: 硫原子在硅酸盐表面发生强化学吸附，且在ASW升华后仍稳定附着；耦合该结果的天文化模型表明，至少10%的分子云总硫储量被锁定在硅酸盐尘埃上。

Conclusion: 硫在硅酸盐尘埃上的化学吸附是导致硫耗尽的关键机制，为‘缺失硫’问题提供了直接的物理成因解释。

Abstract: Sulfur (S) chemistry in the interstellar medium remains poorly constrained due to the longstanding "missing sulfur" problem, where a significant fraction of cosmic sulfur in molecular clouds remains unidentified. To gain a comprehensive understanding of S chemistry in the interstellar medium, its behavior on interstellar dust grains must be clarified. Here, we experimentally investigated the surface elementary processes of S atoms under conditions mimicking interstellar molecular clouds. Our in-situ laser-based detection method enabled the direct tracking of S atoms adsorbed on astrophysically relevant silicate substrates and amorphous solid water (ASW). We found that sulfur atoms strongly chemisorbs onto silicate surfaces and remains bound even after ASW sublimation, rendering it observationally invisible. An astrochemical model coupled with these results suggests that at least 10 percent of the total sulfur budget in molecular clouds must be locked onto silicate dust grains. Our results identify sulfur chemisorption on silicate as a key mechanism driving sulfur depletion and provide a physical explanation for the missing sulfur problem.

</details>


### [165] [Torsionally excited methanol in massive young stellar objects: The role of the mid-IR radiation field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37955)
*C. Sanna,S. Leurini,A. Giannetti,E. Schisano,L. Testi,S. Molinari,J. S. Urquhart,T. Pillai,F. Massi,K. Immer,E. Molinari,S. Casu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了高红移恒星形成区中扭转激发态甲醇（CH3OH）分子的激发机制和空间分布，发现其发射与24微米红外辐射存在幂律关系，可作为被尘埃严重遮蔽区域中嵌入原恒星局部中红外辐射场的有效示踪剂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于高消光和有限分辨率，直接在中红外波段观测大质量恒星形成的最早期阶段十分困难；因此需寻找能间接反映原恒星中红外辐射场的分子谱线探针。

Method: 利用ALMA在890微米波段观测12个处于不同演化阶段的大质量恒星形成云团中的扭转激发态甲醇（vt≥1）转动跃迁，并将其谱线光度（归一化甲醇丰度后）与Spitzer 24微米红外光度进行对比分析。

Result: 扭转激发态甲醇发射普遍致密且与ALMA尘埃连续谱峰值及24微米辐射位置一致；无红外对应体的云团也未探测到vt≥1甲醇发射；vt≥1甲醇谱线光度与24微米光度呈现显著的幂律相关性，且基本不受甲醇丰度变化影响。

Conclusion: CH3OH vt≥1跃迁是探测高度遮蔽区域中嵌入原恒星局部中红外辐射场的有力示踪剂，能提供核心尺度信息；本工作为后续扩大样本统计验证该方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Observing the earliest stages of high-mass star formation in the mid-infrared (mid-IR) is challenging because the extinction is high and the resolution limited. Millimeter vibrationally and torsionally excited transitions of several molecules have been proposed as indirect probes of local mid-IR radiation fields around young stellar objects (YSOs).
  We investigate the excitation and spatial distribution of torsionally excited methanol ($\mathrm{CH_3OH}$) in 12 high-mass star-forming clumps in different evolutionary stages, assessing their potential as tracers of the mid-IR luminosity of embedded protostars.
  We analyzed rotational transitions of methanol within the same torsionally excited states $v_\mathrm{t}$$\geq$1 in the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) 890 $\mathrm{μm}$ observations and compared them with Spitzer 24 $\mathrm{μm}$ data by plotting line luminosities normalized by methanol abundance against mid-IR luminosities.
  The torsionally excited methanol emission is generally compact and systematically coincident with ALMA dust continuum peaks and 24 $\mathrm{μm}$ emission, even in faint sources. The only clump without an IR counterpart also shows no $v_\mathrm{t}$$\geq$1 emission, supporting radiative pumping as the dominant excitation mechanism. We found a promising power-law trend between $v_\mathrm{t}$$\geq$1 methanol lines and 24 $\mathrm{μm}$ luminosities, largely independent of the methanol abundance.
  $\mathrm{CH_3OH}$ $v_\mathrm{t}$$\geq$1 transitions appear to be promising tracers of local mid-IR radiation fields of embedded protostars, providing core-scale information even in highly extincted regions. As a pilot study, our results suggest that extending our study to a larger statistical sample is crucial for testing and improving this method.

</details>


### [166] [Discovery of the Lyman-$α$ Forest in a Star-Forming Galaxy at $z \approx 7.57$: Implications for the Timing and Topology of Cosmic Reionization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38080)
*Jakob M. Helton,Joris Witstok,Marta Laska,Kevin N. Hainline,Nikko J. Cleri,Francesco D'Eugenio,Ignas Juodžbalis,Nimisha Kumari,Joel Leja,Sandro Tacchella,Rachana Bhatawdekar,Andrew J. Bunker,Daniel J. Eisenstein,Zhiyuan Ji,Benjamin D. Johnson,Pierluigi Rinaldi,Brant E. Robertson,Christopher N. A. Willmer*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST观测发现了一个红移z≈7.57的高红移星系JADES-GS-z7-LAF，在莱曼α线蓝端（静止波长约1500 Å）显著探测到逃逸辐射（≈7σ），暗示其视线方向上IGM中中性氢丰度极低（x_HI ≪ 0.1%），挑战了传统对再电离时期电离光子源以矮星系为主的认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙再电离时期的电离光子来源是天体物理关键问题；以往受限于IGM对莱曼α蓝端辐射的强吸收，通常只能间接推断电离光子逃逸率，亟需直接观测证据。

Method: 利用JWST/NIRSpec PRISM光谱（≈7σ探测）与NIRCam F090W成像（≈10σ确认）对高红移星系JADES-GS-z7-LAF进行多波段联合分析，并结合空间位置、光谱形态及前景污染排除等手段验证蓝端辐射的真实性与起源。

Result: 首次在z≈7.57星系中直接探测到莱曼α蓝端通量，表明存在异常透明的IGM视线（x_HI ≪ 0.1%），且该视线与多个高红移星系超密区空间/运动学一致；蓝端连续谱可能源于极端阻尼翼（N_HI≈10^23.3 cm⁻²）或强星云连续谱（贡献60–70%通量）。

Conclusion: 该 massive星系（M_*≈10^9.1 M⊙, M_UV≈−21.5）可能显著贡献再电离电离光子预算，提示大质量星系在宇宙再电离中作用被低估；未来需统计此类源的丰度以修正再电离模型。

Abstract: The escape of ionizing photons from galaxies during the Epoch of Reionization is often inferred indirectly because the intervening neutral hydrogen in the intergalactic medium (IGM) is expected to absorb all flux blueward of Lyman-$α$ at these redshifts. However, in this work, we report the significant detection ($\approx7σ$) of flux blueward of Ly$α$ in a massive ($M_{\ast}\approx10^{9.1}\ M_{\odot}$) star-forming galaxy at $z\approx7.57$, JADES-GS-z7-LAF, using JWST/NIRSpec PRISM spectroscopy. We independently confirm this detection of the Ly$α$ forest (LAF) using JWST/NIRCam F090W imaging ($\approx10σ$). The blueward flux in F090W is spatially coincident with the galaxy and emerges $\approx0.4$ pkpc from its UV centroid at $λ_{\mathrm{rest}}\approx1500\ \mathrm{\mathring{A}}$. We find no evidence for a foreground interloper producing the observed flux, although we cannot formally reject this interpretation with the existing data. The transmitted flux implies an extraordinarily transparent sightline ($x_{\mathrm{HI}}\ll0.1\%$) through the IGM at $z \approx 5.5-7.5$, a sightline that is spatially and kinematically coincident with numerous high-redshift galaxy overdensities, which possibly explains the emergence of this flux. We observe a smooth and gradual turnover around the Ly$α$ break that is either consistent with an extreme damping-wing ($N_{\mathrm{HI}}\approx10^{23.3}\ \mathrm{cm}^{-2}$) or a strong nebular continuum (contributing $\approx60-70\%$ of the flux at $λ_{\mathrm{rest}}=1500\ \mathrm{\mathring{A}}$). JADES-GS-z7-LAF is far more luminous ($M_{\mathrm{UV}}\approx-21.5$, similar to GN-z11) than the faint sources usually invoked as the drivers of cosmic reionization. If such systems are common in the early Universe, massive galaxies may contribute more to the ionizing photon budget than suggested by other indirect constraints.

</details>


### [167] [LYRA: The formation and growth of nuclear star clusters in dwarf galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38179)
*Joaquin Sureda,Azadeh Fattahi,Sownak Bose,Mariya Lyubenova,Jessica E. Doppel,Thales Gutcke,Katja Fahrion,Rüdiger Pakmor*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用LYRA宇宙学流体动力学模拟研究了六个矮星系中核星团（NSCs）的形成与增长，发现NSCs早期即作为致密结构出现，主要通过原位恒星形成（产生大量古老贫金属恒星）和星系并合（通常一次主导）两种途径增长，揭示了矮星系中NSCs形成历史的复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管NSCs的主要增长路径已被广泛研究，但其形成机制仍存在争议，尤其在矮星系中；本文旨在厘清矮星系NSCs的起源与多通道增长历史。

Method: 基于LYRA宇宙学流体动力学模拟，分析六个矮星系样本中NSCs的形成时间、质量增长、恒星来源（原位形成 vs. 并合吸积）及恒星种群性质。

Result: 所有样本星系均形成质量为10^4–10^6 M⊙的NSCs；NSCs早期即致密形成，主质量来自早期原位恒星形成（导致古老贫金属恒星主导），其次为星系并合（常由单次主导）。

Conclusion: 矮星系NSCs的形成是多通道、早期启动的过程，需综合考虑原位形成与并合贡献，单一模型不足以解释其观测特征。

Abstract: Nuclear star clusters (NSCs) are dense massive star clusters ubiquitous in galactic centres across various mass regimes, present in up to $\sim30$ per cent of dwarf galaxies with $M_\star<10^{7}\, \mathrm{M}_\odot$. Although their main growth pathways are well studied, there is still ongoing debate regarding their formation. We use the LYRA cosmological hydrodynamical simulations to study the formation and growth of NSCs in a sample of six dwarf galaxies, where we identify NSCs with masses $M_\mathrm{NSC} \sim 10^4 - 10^6 \, \mathrm{M}_\odot$ in all galaxies in our sample. The NSCs in our sample emerge at early times as a compact component, with the host galaxy growing around them. This is consistent with a scenario in which NSCs are among the earliest structures to form in a galaxy. We characterise the origin of the stars that constitute the NSCs and explore the properties of the stellar populations in each of these channels. We find that most of the NSC mass originates from in-situ star formation within the NSCs themselves. While this might appear at odds with current observations, the bulk of this star formation occurs at early times, resulting in a significant population of old and metal-poor stars, in agreement with an early NSC formation. We also identify galaxy-galaxy mergers as a significant contributor to the NSC mass, with usually one merger dominating this contribution. Our results highlight the complexity in the growth history of NSCs in dwarf galaxies and highlight the importance of considering additional growth channels for NSCs in dwarfs galaxies.

</details>
